/* Pill button that leans toward the cursor and floods with color on hover. */
function CtaButton({
    reduce,
    fine,
    onBoom,
}: {
    reduce: boolean;
    fine: boolean;
    onBoom: (x: number, y: number) => void;
}) {
    const mx = useSpring(0, { stiffness: 220, damping: 16 });
    const my = useSpring(0, { stiffness: 220, damping: 16 });

    function onMove(e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) {
        if (reduce || !fine) return;
        const r = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
        mx.set((e.clientX - (r.left + r.width / 2)) * 0.18);
        my.set((e.clientY - (r.top + r.height / 2)) * 0.28);
    }

    function onLeave() {
        mx.set(0);
        my.set(0);
    }

    return (
        <motion.div
            variants={rise}
            className="mt-[clamp(1rem,3.5vh,2rem)]"
        >
            <motion.a
                href={about.cta.href}
                onClick={(e: MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => onBoom(e.clientX, e.clientY)}
                onPointerMove={onMove}
                onPointerLeave={onLeave}
                whileTap={reduce ? undefined : { scale: 0.95 }}
                style={{ x: mx, y: my }}
                className="group relative inline-flex h-12 items-center gap-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-foreground pl-6 pr-5 text-sm font-medium text-background shadow-sm transition-colors duration-300 hover:text-primary-foreground focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary"
            >
                <span
                    aria-hidden
                    className="absolute inset-0 translate-y-full rounded-full bg-primary transition-transform duration-300 ease-out group-hover:translate-y-0 motion-reduce:transition-none"
                />
                <span className="relative">{about.cta.label}</span>
                <HugeiconsIcon
                    icon={ArrowRight01Icon}
                    size={18}
                    strokeWidth={2}
                    className="relative transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:translate-x-1 motion-reduce:transition-none"
                />
            </motion.a>
        </motion.div>
    );
}

/* ==========================================================================
   MASCOT
   Watches your cursor, blinks, grins on hover, hops on click, and cheers
   when every card has been flipped.
   ========================================================================== */

const MOUTH_REST = "M46 78 Q60 92 74 78 Q60 84 46 78 Z";
const MOUTH_OPEN = "M44 76 Q60 104 76 76 Q60 80 44 76 Z";

function Mascot({
    reduce,
    bubble,
    cheer,
    onPoke,
}: {
    reduce: boolean;
    bubble: string | null;
    cheer: number;
    onPoke: (x: number, y: number) => void;
}) {
    const ref = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
    const controls = useAnimationControls();
    const [hover, setHover] = useState(false);

    const px = useMotionValue(0);
    const py = useMotionValue(0);
    const eyeX = useSpring(px, { stiffness: 220, damping: 18 });
    const eyeY = useSpring(py, { stiffness: 220, damping: 18 });

    useEffect(() => {
        if (reduce) return;

        function onMove(e: PointerEvent) {
            const el = ref.current;
            if (!el) return;
            const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
            const dx = e.clientX - (r.left + r.width / 2);
            const dy = e.clientY - (r.top + r.height / 2);
            const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
            const reach = Math.min(dist / 14, 5);
            px.set((dx / dist) * reach);
            py.set((dy / dist) * reach);
        }

        window.addEventListener("pointermove", onMove);
        return () => window.removeEventListener("pointermove", onMove);
    }, [reduce, px, py]);

    const hop = useCallback(
        (big = false) => {
            if (reduce) return;
            const h = big ? -46 : -30;
            controls.start({
                y: [0, h, 0, h / 5, 0],
                scaleY: [1, 1.1, 0.85, 1.03, 1],
                scaleX: [1, 0.92, 1.1, 0.98, 1],
                rotate: big ? [0, -8, 8, -4, 0] : 0,
                transition: { duration: big ? 0.9 : 0.6, times: [0, 0.35, 0.6, 0.8, 1], ease: "easeOut" },
            });
        },
        [controls, reduce],
    );

    useEffect(() => {
        if (cheer > 0) hop(true);
    }, [cheer, hop]);

    function poke(e: MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) {
        onPoke(e.clientX, e.clientY);
        hop();
    }

    const eyeStyle: CSSProperties = {
        transformBox: "fill-box",
        transformOrigin: "center",
    };

    const happy = hover || cheer > 0;

    return (
        <div className="relative shrink-0">
            <AnimatePresence>
                {bubble && (
                    <motion.div
                        key={bubble}
                        role="status"
                        initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 8, scale: 0.9 }}
                        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
                        exit={reduce ? undefined : { opacity: 0, y: 6, scale: 0.92 }}
                        transition={spring}
                        className="absolute bottom-full right-0 z-40 mb-1 w-max max-w-[10rem] origin-bottom-right rounded-2xl bg-foreground px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium leading-5 text-background shadow-lg sm:right-2 sm:max-w-[11rem]"
                    >
                        {bubble}
                        <span
                            aria-hidden
                            className="absolute -bottom-1 right-6 size-2.5 rotate-45 bg-foreground"
                        />
                    </motion.div>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>

            <motion.button
                ref={ref}
                type="button"
                aria-label="Poke the blob"
                onClick={poke}
                onHoverStart={() => setHover(true)}
                onHoverEnd={() => setHover(false)}
                whileHover={reduce ? undefined : { rotate: -4, scale: 1.06 }}
                whileTap={reduce ? undefined : { scale: 0.92 }}
                transition={spring}
                className="block w-14 cursor-pointer rounded-full focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary sm:w-20 lg:w-[5.5rem]"
            >
                <motion.svg
                    viewBox="0 -12 120 124"
                    animate={controls}
                    className="block h-auto w-full overflow-visible"
                    style={{ transformOrigin: "50% 100%" }}
                >
                    <path
                        d="M60 8 Q56 -2 66 -6"
                        fill="none"
                        stroke="var(--primary)"
                        strokeWidth="4"
                        strokeLinecap="round"
                    />
                    <circle cx="66" cy="-6" r="5.5" fill={PILLARS[0].tint} />

                    <path
                        d="M60 8 C92 8 112 32 112 62 C112 90 92 104 60 104 C28 104 8 90 8 62 C8 32 28 8 60 8 Z"
                        fill="var(--primary)"
                    />

                    <circle cx="27" cy="72" r="7" fill={PILLARS[2].tint} opacity="0.55" />
                    <circle cx="93" cy="72" r="7" fill={PILLARS[2].tint} opacity="0.55" />

                    {[42, 78].map((cx) => (
                        <motion.g
                            key={cx}
                            style={eyeStyle}
                            animate={reduce ? undefined : { scaleY: [1, 1, 0.1, 1] }}
                            transition={{
                                duration: 3.6,
                                times: [0, 0.92, 0.96, 1],
                                repeat: Infinity,
                            }}
                        >
                            <circle cx={cx} cy="54" r="13" fill="var(--background)" />
                            <motion.circle
                                cx={cx}
                                cy="54"
                                r="6"
                                fill="var(--foreground)"
                                style={{ x: eyeX, y: eyeY }}
                            />
                        </motion.g>
                    ))}

                    <motion.path
                        fill="var(--foreground)"
                        initial={false}
                        animate={{ d: happy ? MOUTH_OPEN : MOUTH_REST }}
                        transition={spring}
                    />
                </motion.svg>
            </motion.button>
        </div>
    );
}

/* ==========================================================================
   STICKER CARD
   Drag it, tilt it with the cursor, click it to flip it.
   Keyboard: Enter / Space flips, arrow keys nudge.
   ========================================================================== */

function StickerCard({
    pillar,
    index,
    boardRef,
    canDrag,
    reduce,
    tidyTick,
    isTop,
    flipped,
    onFlip,
    onFront,
}: {
    pillar: Pillar;
    index: number;
    boardRef: RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>;
    canDrag: boolean;
    reduce: boolean;
    tidyTick: number;
    isTop: boolean;
    flipped: boolean;
    onFlip: (next: boolean) => void;
    onFront: () => void;
}) {
    const x = useMotionValue(0);
    const y = useMotionValue(0);
    const [dragging, setDragging] = useState(false);
    const moved = useRef(false);

    // lean into the direction you're dragging, like a real sticker would
    const rotate = useTransform(x, (v) => pillar.tilt + clamp(v / 40, -8, 8));

    // 3D tilt + glare that follow the cursor over the card
    const gx = useMotionValue(50);
    const gy = useMotionValue(50);
    const rx = useSpring(useTransform(gy, [0, 100], [7, -7]), tiltSpring);
    const ry = useSpring(useTransform(gx, [0, 100], [-9, 9]), tiltSpring);
    const glare = useMotionTemplate`radial-gradient(circle at ${gx}% ${gy}%, color-mix(in oklab, white 38%, transparent), transparent 55%)`;

    function onPointerMove(e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
        if (!canDrag || reduce || dragging) return;
        const r = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
        gx.set(clamp(((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width) * 100, 0, 100));
        gy.set(clamp(((e.clientY - r.top) / r.height) * 100, 0, 100));
    }

    function onPointerLeave() {
        gx.set(50);
        gy.set(50);
    }

    // "Tidy up" glides every card home, one after another
    const lastTick = useRef(tidyTick);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (lastTick.current === tidyTick) return;
        lastTick.current = tidyTick;
        const t = reduce ? { duration: 0 } : { ...softSpring, delay: index * 0.06 };
        animate(x, 0, t);
        animate(y, 0, t);
    }, [tidyTick, x, y, reduce, index]);

    function toggle() {
        onFront();
        onFlip(!flipped);
    }

    function onTap() {
        if (moved.current) {
            moved.current = false;
            return;
        }
        toggle();
    }

    function onKeyDown(e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
        if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
            e.preventDefault();
            toggle();
            return;
        }
        const step = 16;
        const lim = 70;
        const t = reduce ? { duration: 0 } : spring;
        const nudge: Record<string, [number, number]> = {
            ArrowLeft: [-step, 0],
            ArrowRight: [step, 0],
            ArrowUp: [0, -step],
            ArrowDown: [0, step],
        };
        const d = nudge[e.key];
        if (!d) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        onFront();
        animate(x, clamp(x.get() + d[0], -lim, lim), t);
        animate(y, clamp(y.get() + d[1], -lim, lim), t);
    }

    const tintVars = { "--tint": pillar.tint } as CSSProperties;
    const faceStyle: CSSProperties = {
        backfaceVisibility: "hidden",
        WebkitBackfaceVisibility: "hidden",
    };

    const glareLayer = (
        <motion.span
            aria-hidden
            style={{ background: glare }}
            className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 rounded-3xl opacity-0 mix-blend-soft-light transition-opacity duration-200 group-hover:opacity-100"
        />
    );

    return (
        <motion.li
            variants={drop}
            style={{ ...tintVars, zIndex: isTop ? 30 : 1 }}
            className={`relative h-full min-h-0 ${index % 2 === 1 ? "sm:mt-4 sm:-mb-4" : ""}`}
        >
            <motion.div
                role="button"
                tabIndex={0}
                aria-pressed={flipped}
                aria-label={
                    flipped
                        ? `${pillar.backTitle}. ${pillar.backText} Press to flip back.`
                        : `${pillar.title}. ${pillar.text} Press to flip the card.`
                }
                onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
                onTap={onTap}
                onPointerMove={onPointerMove}
                onPointerLeave={onPointerLeave}
                drag={canDrag}
                dragConstraints={boardRef}
                dragElastic={0.15}
                dragMomentum={false}
                onDragStart={() => {
                    moved.current = true;
                    setDragging(true);
                    onFront();
                }}
                onDragEnd={() => {
                    setDragging(false);
                    window.setTimeout(() => {
                        moved.current = false;
                    }, 60);
                }}
                variants={cardHover}
                whileHover={reduce ? undefined : "hover"}
                whileDrag={{ scale: 1.06 }}
                whileTap={{ scale: 0.97 }}
                transition={spring}
                style={{ x, y, rotate }}
                className={`group h-full rounded-3xl outline-none [perspective:1100px] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[color:var(--tint)] focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-background ${
                    canDrag ? "cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing" : "cursor-pointer"
                } ${dragging ? "drop-shadow-2xl" : "drop-shadow-sm"}`}
            >
                {/* cursor tilt */}
                <motion.div
                    style={{ rotateX: reduce ? 0 : rx, rotateY: reduce ? 0 : ry, transformStyle: "preserve-3d" }}
                    className="h-full w-full"
                >
                    {/* flipper */}
                    <motion.div
                        animate={{ rotateY: flipped ? 180 : 0 }}
                        transition={reduce ? { duration: 0 } : softSpring}
                        style={{ transformStyle: "preserve-3d" }}
                        className="relative h-full w-full"
                    >
                        {/* FRONT */}
                        <div
                            aria-hidden
                            style={faceStyle}
                            className="absolute inset-0 flex select-none flex-col rounded-3xl border border-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_32%,transparent)] bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_9%,var(--background))] p-3.5 sm:p-5 lg:p-6"
                        >
                            <span
                                aria-hidden
                                className="absolute -top-2.5 left-1/2 h-5 w-14 -translate-x-1/2 -rotate-3 rounded-[3px] bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_38%,transparent)] backdrop-blur-sm sm:-top-3 sm:h-6 sm:w-16"
                            />

                            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                                <motion.div
                                    variants={iconHover}
                                    className="flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_20%,transparent)] text-[color:var(--tint)] sm:size-11 sm:rounded-2xl"
                                >
                                    <HugeiconsIcon icon={pillar.icon} size={22} strokeWidth={1.7} />
                                </motion.div>

                                <span className="hidden rounded-full border border-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_32%,transparent)] px-2.5 py-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground sm:inline">
                                    {pillar.kicker}
                                </span>
                            </div>

                            <div className="mt-auto">
                                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold leading-tight tracking-tight sm:text-lg lg:text-xl">
                                    {pillar.title}
                                </h3>
                                <p className="mt-2 hidden text-[13px] leading-5 text-muted-foreground sm:line-clamp-3 sm:block lg:line-clamp-4 lg:text-sm lg:leading-6">
                                    {pillar.text}
                                </p>
                                <span className="mt-2 hidden items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-[color:var(--tint)] sm:inline-flex">
                                    Click to flip
                                    <HugeiconsIcon icon={ArrowRight01Icon} size={14} strokeWidth={2} />
                                </span>
                            </div>
                            {glareLayer}
                        </div>

                        {/* BACK */}
                        <div
                            aria-hidden
                            style={{ ...faceStyle, transform: "rotateY(180deg)" }}
                            className="absolute inset-0 flex select-none flex-col items-start rounded-3xl border border-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_45%,transparent)] bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_22%,var(--background))] p-3.5 sm:p-5 lg:p-6"
                        >
                            <motion.span
                                variants={emojiHover}
                                className="block text-3xl leading-none sm:text-4xl lg:text-5xl"
                            >
                                {pillar.emoji}
                            </motion.span>

                            <h3 className="mt-2 text-sm font-semibold leading-tight tracking-tight sm:mt-4 sm:text-base lg:text-lg">
                                {pillar.backTitle}
                            </h3>
                            <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-4 text-foreground/80 sm:text-[13px] sm:leading-5 lg:text-sm lg:leading-6">
                                {pillar.backText}
                            </p>

                            <span className="mt-1 hidden text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground sm:block">
                                Click to flip back
                            </span>
                            {glareLayer}
                        </div>
                    </motion.div>
                </motion.div>
            </motion.div>
        </motion.li>
    );
}

/* ==========================================================================
   BOARD TOOLBAR
   ========================================================================== */

function Toolbar({
    flipped,
    fine,
    reduce,
    tidyTick,
    onTidy,
    bubble,
    cheer,
    onPoke,
}: {
    flipped: Record<string, boolean>;
    fine: boolean;
    reduce: boolean;
    tidyTick: number;
    onTidy: () => void;
    bubble: string | null;
    cheer: number;
    onPoke: (x: number, y: number) => void;
}) {
    const count = PILLARS.filter((p) => flipped[p.id]).length;
    const done = count === PILLARS.length;

    return (
        <div className="mt-3 flex shrink-0 items-end justify-between gap-3 lg:mt-4">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 lg:gap-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <ul aria-hidden className="flex gap-1.5">
                        {PILLARS.map((p) => (
                            <li
                                key={p.id}
                                className="relative size-3 rounded-full border border-border bg-muted"
                            >
                                <motion.span
                                    initial={false}
                                    animate={{ scale: flipped[p.id] ? 1 : 0 }}
                                    transition={reduce ? { duration: 0 } : spring}
                                    className="absolute -inset-px rounded-full"
                                    style={{ background: p.tint }}
                                />
                            </li>
                        ))}
                    </ul>
                    <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground" aria-live="polite">
                        {done ? "You found them all!" : `${count} of ${PILLARS.length} flipped`}
                    </p>
                </div>

                <p className="hidden max-w-[16rem] text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground sm:block">
                    {fine
                        ? "Drag the cards around. Click one to flip it."
                        : "Tap a card to flip it."}
                </p>

                <motion.button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={onTidy}
                    whileHover={reduce ? undefined : { y: -2 }}
                    whileTap={reduce ? undefined : { scale: 0.94 }}
                    transition={spring}
                    className="inline-flex h-9 w-fit items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-border bg-background px-4 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-muted/60 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary sm:h-10"
                >
                    <motion.span
                        aria-hidden
                        animate={{ rotate: tidyTick * 360 }}
                        transition={reduce ? { duration: 0 } : softSpring}
                        className="flex"
                    >
                        <HugeiconsIcon icon={RefreshIcon} size={16} strokeWidth={2} />
                    </motion.span>
                    Tidy up
                </motion.button>
            </div>

            <Mascot reduce={reduce} bubble={bubble} cheer={cheer} onPoke={onPoke} />
        </div>
    );
}

/* ==========================================================================
   SECTION
   Exactly one screen: never taller or wider than the viewport.
   ========================================================================== */

export default function About() {
    const reduce = !!useReducedMotion();
    const fine = useFinePointer();
    const initial = reduce ? "show" : "hidden";

    const sectionRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
    const boardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const { bursts, fire } = useConfetti(!reduce);

    const [flipped, setFlipped] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
    const [topId, setTopId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [tidyTick, setTidyTick] = useState(0);
    const [cheer, setCheer] = useState(0);

    // cursor spotlight
    const sx = useSpring(-999, { stiffness: 140, damping: 20 });
    const sy = useSpring(-999, { stiffness: 140, damping: 20 });

    function onSectionMove(e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLElement>) {
        if (!fine || reduce) return;
        const r = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
        sx.set(e.clientX - r.left);
        sy.set(e.clientY - r.top);
    }

    function onSectionLeave() {
        sx.set(-999);
        sy.set(-999);
    }

    // speech bubble for the mascot
    const [bubble, setBubble] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const bubbleTimer = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);
    const pokes = useRef(0);

    const say = useCallback((text: string) => {
        setBubble(text);
        window.clearTimeout(bubbleTimer.current);
        bubbleTimer.current = window.setTimeout(() => setBubble(null), 2800);
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => () => window.clearTimeout(bubbleTimer.current), []);

    const poke = useCallback(
        (x: number, y: number) => {
            fire(x, y, 14);
            say(MASCOT_LINES[pokes.current++ % MASCOT_LINES.length]);
        },
        [fire, say],
    );

    // easter egg: flip every card and the board celebrates
    const allFlipped = PILLARS.every((p) => flipped[p.id]);
    const wasAll = useRef(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (allFlipped && !wasAll.current) {
            const r = boardRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
            if (r) {
                fire(r.left + r.width / 2, r.top + r.height / 2, 36);
            }
            setCheer((c) => c + 1);
            say("You found them all! 🎉");
        }
        wasAll.current = allFlipped;
    }, [allFlipped, fire, say]);

    function tidy() {
        setTidyTick((t) => t + 1);
        setFlipped({});
        setTopId(null);
        say("Ahh, much better.");
    }

    function flip(p: Pillar, next: boolean) {
        setFlipped((f) => ({ ...f, [p.id]: next }));
        if (next) say(`${p.emoji} ${p.backTitle}`);
    }

    return (
        <section
            id="about"
            ref={sectionRef}
            onPointerMove={onSectionMove}
            onPointerLeave={onSectionLeave}
            className="relative box-border flex min-h-svh w-full max-w-[100vw] flex-col overflow-x-clip lg:h-svh lg:max-h-svh lg:overflow-hidden border-t border-border bg-background px-4 pb-[clamp(0.75rem,3vh,2rem)] pt-[clamp(4.5rem,11vh,6.5rem)] sm:px-6"
        >
            <div
                aria-hidden
                className="pointer-events-none absolute -left-32 top-10 size-[420px] rounded-full bg-primary/10 blur-[110px]"
            />
            <Spotlight sx={sx} sy={sy} />
            <Shapes reduce={reduce} />

            <div className="relative mx-auto grid min-h-0 w-full max-w-6xl flex-1 grid-rows-[auto_auto] gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[5fr_7fr] lg:grid-rows-1 lg:items-center lg:gap-16">
                {/* ---------- left: the words ---------- */}
                <motion.div
                    initial={initial}
                    whileInView="show"
                    viewport={{ once: true, amount: 0.3 }}
                    variants={stack}
                    className="flex min-h-0 flex-col items-start"
                >
                    <motion.div variants={rise} className="mb-[clamp(0.5rem,2vh,1.25rem)]">
                        <Greeting reduce={reduce} />
                    </motion.div>

                    <Headline reduce={reduce} toptext={about.headingTop} bottomtext={about.headingBottom} />

                    <div className="mt-[clamp(0.75rem,3vh,2rem)] flex max-w-md flex-col gap-[clamp(0.5rem,1.6vh,1rem)]">
                        <motion.p
                            variants={rise}
                            className="text-base leading-7 text-muted-foreground"
                        >
                            {about.paragraph}
                        </motion.p>

                        <motion.p variants={rise} className="text-sm leading-6 sm:text-base sm:leading-7">
                            {about.half}
                        </motion.p>

                        <motion.div variants={rise}>
                            <Currently reduce={reduce} />
                        </motion.div>
                    </div>

                    <CtaButton reduce={reduce} fine={fine} onBoom={(x, y) => fire(x, y, 22)} />
                </motion.div>

                {/* ---------- right: the playground ---------- */}
                <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col lg:max-h-[44rem]">
                    <div
                        ref={boardRef}
                        className="relative min-h-[26rem] flex-1 rounded-[1.5rem] sm:min-h-[30rem] lg:min-h-0 border border-dashed border-border p-3 sm:rounded-[2rem] sm:p-5"
                        style={{
                            backgroundImage:
                                "radial-gradient(color-mix(in oklab, var(--foreground) 14%, transparent) 1px, transparent 1px)",
                            backgroundSize: "22px 22px",
                        }}
                    >
                        <motion.ul
                            initial={initial}
                            whileInView="show"
                            viewport={{ once: true, amount: 0.3 }}
                            variants={boardStack}
                            className="grid h-full grid-cols-2 grid-rows-2 gap-3 sm:gap-5"
                        >
                            {PILLARS.map((pillar, i) => (
                                <StickerCard
                                    key={pillar.id}
                                    pillar={pillar}
                                    index={i}
                                    boardRef={boardRef}
                                    canDrag={fine}
                                    reduce={reduce}
                                    tidyTick={tidyTick}
                                    isTop={topId === pillar.id}
                                    flipped={!!flipped[pillar.id]}
                                    onFlip={(next) => flip(pillar, next)}
                                    onFront={() => setTopId(pillar.id)}
                                />
                            ))}
                        </motion.ul>
                    </div>

                    <Toolbar
                        flipped={flipped}
                        fine={fine}
                        reduce={reduce}
                        tidyTick={tidyTick}
                        onTidy={tidy}
                        bubble={bubble}
                        cheer={cheer}
                        onPoke={poke}
                    />
                </div>
            </div>

            <ConfettiLayer bursts={bursts} />
        </section>
    );
}

========================================================================
FILEPATH: ./src/components/section/contact/contact.tsx

"use client";
// redesign.sh:v1

import { useRef, useState, type CSSProperties, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { AnimatePresence, motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
import ConfettiLayer from "@/components/ui/ConfettiLayer";
import Eyebrow from "@/components/ui/Eyebrow";
import Headline from "@/components/ui/Headline";
import Screen from "@/components/ui/Screen";
import { site } from "@/content/site";
import { useConfetti } from "@/hooks/useConfetti";
import { TINTS } from "@/lib/tokens";
import { easeOutExpo, fade, pop, spring, stackLoose, viewportOnce } from "@/lib/motion";

/* The mood is a postage stamp. Its id is what /api/contact expects. */
const MOODS = [
    { id: "idea", emoji: "💡", label: "I have an idea", short: "Idea", tint: TINTS.amber },
    { id: "job", emoji: "💼", label: "I have a job", short: "Job", tint: TINTS.sky },
    { id: "team", emoji: "🤝", label: "Let's team up", short: "Team", tint: TINTS.green },
    { id: "hi", emoji: "👋", label: "Just saying hi", short: "Hello", tint: TINTS.rose },
] as const;

type Mood = (typeof MOODS)[number];
type Status = "idle" | "sending" | "sent" | "error";

const EMAIL_RE = /^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/;
const MAX_MESSAGE = 1200;
const ERR = "text-[oklch(0.62_0.2_25)]";

const line =
    "w-full border-0 border-b border-foreground/30 bg-transparent py-1.5 text-base outline-none transition-colors placeholder:text-muted-foreground/60 focus:border-foreground aria-[invalid=true]:border-[oklch(0.62_0.2_25)]";

function Stamp({ mood, className = "" }: { mood: Mood; className?: string }) {
    return (
        <span
            style={{ "--tint": mood.tint } as CSSProperties}
            className={`relative inline-flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 border border-dashed border-[color:var(--tint)] bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_14%,var(--ed-paper))] p-1.5 ${className}`}
        >
            <span aria-hidden className="text-2xl leading-none">
                {mood.emoji}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold leading-none text-[color:var(--tint)]">{mood.short}</span>
        </span>
    );
}

function Postmark({ className = "" }: { className?: string }) {
    return (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" aria-hidden className={className} fill="none" stroke="currentColor">
            <defs>
                <path id="ed-postmark-arc" d="M50 50 m-38 0 a38 38 0 1 1 76 0 a38 38 0 1 1 -76 0" />
            </defs>
            <circle cx="50" cy="50" r="46" strokeWidth="1.5" />
            <circle cx="50" cy="50" r="30" strokeWidth="1" />
            <text fontSize="8.5" letterSpacing="2.2" fill="currentColor" stroke="none" fontFamily="ui-monospace, monospace">
                <textPath href="#ed-postmark-arc">DELHI NCR · REPLIES IN A DAY · </textPath>
            </text>
            <path d="M24 44 q7 -5 13 0 t13 0 t13 0 M24 54 q7 -5 13 0 t13 0 t13 0" strokeWidth="1" />
        </svg>
    );
}

function CopyEmail() {
    const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

    async function copy() {
        try {
            await navigator.clipboard.writeText(site.email);
        } catch {
            /* clipboard blocked, the mailto link still works */
        }
        setCopied(true);
        window.setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1800);
    }

    return (
        <Button onClick={copy} variant="outline">
            <span aria-live="polite">{copied ? "Copied" : site.email}</span>
        </Button>
    );
}

export default function Contact() {
    const reduce = !!useReducedMotion();
    const cardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const { bursts, fire } = useConfetti(!reduce);

    const [moodId, setMoodId] = useState<Mood["id"]>("idea");
    const [name, setName] = useState("");
    const [from, setFrom] = useState("");
    const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
    const [trap, setTrap] = useState("");
    const [status, setStatus] = useState<Status>("idle");
    const [showErrors, setShowErrors] = useState(false);

    const mood = MOODS.find((m) => m.id === moodId) ?? MOODS[0];

    const errors = {
        name: name.trim().length < 2 ? "What should I call you?" : "",
        email: EMAIL_RE.test(from.trim()) ? "" : "That email looks a bit off",
        message: message.trim().length < 10 ? "A few more words, please" : "",
    };
    const valid = !errors.name && !errors.email && !errors.message;
    const shown = (key: keyof typeof errors) => (showErrors ? errors[key] : "");

    const mailto = () => {
        const subject = encodeURIComponent(`${mood.emoji} ${mood.label}`);
        const body = encodeURIComponent(`${message}\n\n${name} (${from})`);
        return `mailto:${site.email}?subject=${subject}&body=${body}`;
    };

    async function onSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
        e.preventDefault();
        setShowErrors(true);
        if (!valid || status === "sending") return;
        setStatus("sending");
        const rect = cardRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
        try {
            const res = await fetch("/api/contact", {
                method: "POST",
                headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    mood: mood.id,
                    name: name.trim(),
                    email: from.trim(),
                    message: message.trim(),
                    website: trap,
                }),
            });
            if (!res.ok) throw new Error(String(res.status));
            setStatus("sent");
            if (rect) fire(rect.left + rect.width / 2, rect.top + rect.height / 2, 36);
        } catch {
            setStatus("error");
        }
    }

    function reset() {
        setName("");
        setFrom("");
        setMessage("");
        setTrap("");
        setShowErrors(false);
        setStatus("idle");
    }

    return (
        <Screen id="contact">
            <div className="mx-auto grid w-full max-w-6xl flex-1 content-center gap-12 px-4 pb-12 pt-[clamp(4.5rem,11vh,6.5rem)] sm:px-6 lg:grid-cols-[5fr_7fr] lg:items-center lg:gap-16">
                {/* ---------- left: the words ---------- */}
                <motion.div
                    initial={reduce ? "show" : "hidden"}
                    whileInView="show"
                    viewport={viewportOnce}
                    variants={stackLoose}
                    className="flex flex-col items-start gap-[clamp(0.75rem,2.6vh,1.5rem)]"
                >
                    <motion.div variants={fade}>
                        <Eyebrow index="05">Contact</Eyebrow>
                    </motion.div>

                    <Headline reduce={reduce} size="xl" toptext="Got an idea?" bottomtext="Say hi." />

                    <motion.p variants={fade} className="max-w-md text-base leading-7 text-muted-foreground">
                        Pick a stamp, write a few lines and post it. I read every message myself and usually reply within a day.
                    </motion.p>

                    <motion.div variants={fade}>
                        <CopyEmail />
                    </motion.div>

                    <motion.ul variants={fade} className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-6 gap-y-2">
                        {site.social.map((l) => (
                            <li key={l.label}>
                                <a
                                    href={l.href}
                                    target="_blank"
                                    rel="noreferrer noopener"
                                    className="ed-link rounded-sm text-sm font-medium outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary"
                                >
                                    {l.label} <span aria-hidden>↗</span>
                                </a>
                            </li>
                        ))}
                    </motion.ul>
                </motion.div>

                {/* ---------- right: the letter ---------- */}
                <motion.div
                    initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 36, rotate: 1.2 }}
                    whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, rotate: 0 }}
                    viewport={{ once: true, amount: 0.2 }}
                    transition={{ ...spring, delay: 0.08 }}
                    className="w-full"
                >
                    <AnimatePresence mode="wait" initial={false}>
                        {status === "sent" ? (
                            <motion.div
                                key="sent"
                                initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 12 }}
                                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                                exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
                                className="ed-paper flex min-h-[22rem] flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4 px-8 py-10 text-center"
                            >
                                <svg viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden className="size-28 -rotate-6 text-primary">
                                    <motion.circle
                                        cx="60"
                                        cy="60"
                                        r="52"
                                        fill="none"
                                        stroke="currentColor"
                                        strokeWidth="1.5"
                                        initial={reduce ? false : { pathLength: 0 }}
                                        animate={{ pathLength: 1 }}
                                        transition={{ duration: 1, ease: easeOutExpo }}
                                    />
                                    <text x="60" y="64" textAnchor="middle" fontSize="11" letterSpacing="3" fill="currentColor" fontFamily="ui-monospace, monospace">
                                        DELIVERED
                                    </text>
                                </svg>
                                <h3 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">
                                    Thanks, <span className="ds-serif text-primary">{name.trim().split(" ")[0]}.</span>
                                </h3>
                                <p className="max-w-xs text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground">Your letter is on its way. I&apos;ll write back soon.</p>
                                <Button onClick={reset} variant="outline" size="sm">
                                    Write another letter
                                </Button>
                            </motion.div>
                        ) : (
                            <motion.form
                                key="form"
                                noValidate
                                onSubmit={onSubmit}
                                exit={
                                    reduce
                                        ? { opacity: 0 }
                                        : { opacity: 0, x: 240, y: -140, rotate: 10, scale: 0.5, transition: { duration: 0.55, ease: "easeIn" } }
                                }
                                className="relative"
                            >
                                <motion.div
                                    ref={cardRef}
                                    animate={status === "sending" && !reduce ? { y: [0, -3, 0], transition: { duration: 0.9, repeat: Infinity } } : { y: 0 }}
                                    className="ed-paper pb-8 pl-14 pr-6 pt-8 sm:pl-[4.25rem] sm:pr-10 sm:pt-10"
                                >
                                    {/* stamp + postmark, top right */}
                                    <div className="absolute right-4 top-4 h-[4.6rem] w-[3.8rem] sm:right-6 sm:top-6">
                                        <AnimatePresence mode="popLayout" initial={false}>
                                            <motion.div
                                                key={mood.id}
                                                initial={reduce ? false : { y: -60, scale: 1.6, rotate: -20, opacity: 0 }}
                                                animate={{ y: 0, scale: 1, rotate: 4, opacity: 1 }}
                                                exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 16, transition: { duration: 0.12 } }}
                                                transition={pop}
                                                className="h-full w-full"
                                            >
                                                <Stamp mood={mood} className="h-full w-full" />
                                            </motion.div>
                                        </AnimatePresence>
                                        <Postmark className="pointer-events-none absolute -left-12 top-1 size-20 -rotate-12 text-foreground/30" />
                                    </div>

                                    <p className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">{site.location}</p>

                                    <div className="mt-6 flex items-baseline justify-between gap-4 pr-20 sm:pr-24">
                                        <label htmlFor="c-msg" className="ds-serif text-3xl italic">
                                            Dear Raunak,
                                        </label>
                                    </div>

                                    <textarea
                                        id="c-msg"
                                        value={message}
                                        onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder="Tell me a little about it..."
                                        maxLength={MAX_MESSAGE}
                                        aria-invalid={!!shown("message")}
                                        aria-describedby={shown("message") ? "c-msg-error" : undefined}
                                        className="ed-ruled mt-3 block h-[12rem] w-full resize-none bg-transparent text-[15px] outline-none placeholder:text-muted-foreground/60"
                                    />
                                    <div className="mt-1 flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                                        <div className="min-h-4">
                                            {shown("message") && (
                                                <p id="c-msg-error" role="alert" className={`text-xs ${ERR}`}>
                                                    {shown("message")}
                                                </p>
                                            )}
                                        </div>
                                        <span className="text-xs tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">
                                            {message.length}/{MAX_MESSAGE}
                                        </span>
                                    </div>

                                    <p className="ds-serif mt-5 text-xl italic">Yours,</p>
                                    <div className="mt-2 grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
                                        <div>
                                            <label htmlFor="c-name" className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                                                From
                                            </label>
                                            <input
                                                id="c-name"
                                                value={name}
                                                onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                                                placeholder="Ada Lovelace"
                                                autoComplete="name"
                                                maxLength={100}
                                                aria-invalid={!!shown("name")}
                                                aria-describedby={shown("name") ? "c-name-error" : undefined}
                                                className={line}
                                            />
                                            {shown("name") && (
                                                <p id="c-name-error" role="alert" className={`mt-1 text-xs ${ERR}`}>
                                                    {shown("name")}
                                                </p>
                                            )}
                                        </div>
                                        <div>
                                            <label htmlFor="c-email" className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                                                Reply to
                                            </label>
                                            <input
                                                id="c-email"
                                                type="email"
                                                value={from}
                                                onChange={(e) => setFrom(e.target.value)}
                                                placeholder="ada@example.com"
                                                autoComplete="email"
                                                maxLength={200}
                                                aria-invalid={!!shown("email")}
                                                aria-describedby={shown("email") ? "c-email-error" : undefined}
                                                className={line}
                                            />
                                            {shown("email") && (
                                                <p id="c-email-error" role="alert" className={`mt-1 text-xs ${ERR}`}>
                                                    {shown("email")}
                                                </p>
                                            )}
                                        </div>
                                    </div>

                                    {/* Honeypot. People never see or fill this. */}
                                    <input
                                        type="text"
                                        name="website"
                                        value={trap}
                                        onChange={(e) => setTrap(e.target.value)}
                                        tabIndex={-1}
                                        autoComplete="off"
                                        aria-hidden
                                        className="absolute -left-[9999px] size-0 opacity-0"
                                    />
                                </motion.div>

                                {/* stamp tray + send */}
                                <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
                                    <fieldset className="min-w-0">
                                        <legend className="mb-2 text-sm font-medium">Pick a stamp</legend>
                                        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2.5">
                                            {MOODS.map((m, i) => {
                                                const on = m.id === moodId;
                                                return (
                                                    <motion.button
                                                        key={m.id}
                                                        type="button"
                                                        aria-pressed={on}
                                                        aria-label={m.label}
                                                        title={m.label}
                                                        onClick={() => setMoodId(m.id)}
                                                        whileHover={reduce ? undefined : { y: -3, rotate: i % 2 ? 3 : -3 }}
                                                        whileTap={reduce ? undefined : { scale: 0.94 }}
                                                        animate={reduce ? undefined : { y: on ? -5 : 0 }}
                                                        transition={pop}
                                                        className={`rounded-sm outline-none transition-opacity focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-background ${
                                                            on ? "drop-shadow-md" : "opacity-70 hover:opacity-100"
                                                        }`}
                                                    >
                                                        <Stamp mood={m} className="h-14 w-12" />
                                                    </motion.button>
                                                );
                                            })}
                                        </div>
                                    </fieldset>

                                    <Button type="submit" variant="solid" loading={status === "sending"}>
                                        {status === "sending" ? "Posting..." : "Post it"}
                                    </Button>
                                </div>

                                {status === "error" && (
                                    <p role="alert" className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                                        That didn&apos;t go through, so the letter is still here.{" "}
                                        <a href={mailto()} className="font-medium text-primary underline underline-offset-4">
                                            Send it from your email app instead
                                        </a>
                                        .
                                    </p>
                                )}
                            </motion.form>
                        )}
                    </AnimatePresence>
                </motion.div>
            </div>

            <ConfettiLayer bursts={bursts} />
        </Screen>
    );
}

FILEPATH: ./src/components/section/experience/Experience.tsx

"use client";
// redesign.sh:v1

import { useRef } from "react";
import { motion, useReducedMotion, useScroll, useTransform, type MotionValue } from "framer-motion";
import Reveal from "@/components/motion/Reveal";
import SectionHeader from "@/components/ui/SectionHeader";
import { copy, education, experience, writing } from "@/content/experience";

type Job = (typeof experience)[number];

/** One point of the job. Its dot fills and its text brightens as the rail reaches it. */
function Point({ text, i, n, progress, reduce }: { text: string; i: number; n: number; progress: MotionValue<number>; reduce: boolean }) {
    const a = (i / n) * 0.8;
    const b = a + 0.18;
    const o = useTransform(progress, [a, b], [0.35, 1]);
    const s = useTransform(progress, [a, b], [0, 1]);
    return (
        <li className="relative grid grid-cols-[2.5rem_1fr] items-start gap-x-3">
            <span className="relative flex justify-center pt-[0.7rem]">
                <span className="absolute size-2.5 rounded-full border border-border bg-background" />
                <motion.span style={reduce ? undefined : { scale: s }} className="relative size-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary" />
            </span>
            <motion.p style={reduce ? undefined : { opacity: o }} className="text-lg leading-8 sm:text-xl sm:leading-9">
                <span className="mr-3 align-middle font-mono text-xs tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">{String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</span>
                {text}
            </motion.p>
        </li>
    );
}

function Chapter({ job, index, total, reduce }: { job: Job; index: number; total: number; reduce: boolean }) {
    const article = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
    const list = useRef<HTMLUListElement>(null);

    const { scrollYProgress: whole } = useScroll({ target: article, offset: ["start end", "end start"] });
    const numY = useTransform(whole, [0, 1], ["-8%", "14%"]);

    const { scrollYProgress: rail } = useScroll({ target: list, offset: ["start 75%", "end 55%"] });

    return (
        <article ref={article} className="grid gap-8 border-t border-border pt-10 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,4fr)_minmax(0,8fr)] lg:gap-14 lg:pt-14">
            {/* ---------- the year, as typography ---------- */}
            <div className="relative lg:sticky lg:top-28 lg:self-start">
                <p className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">
                    {String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")} / {String(total).padStart(2, "0")}
                </p>
                <motion.p
                    aria-label={job.period}
                    style={reduce ? undefined : { y: numY }}
                    className="ed-outline-text mt-2 text-[clamp(4.5rem,9.5vw,9rem)] font-semibold leading-[0.9] tracking-tight [overflow-wrap:anywhere]"
                >
                    {job.period}
                </motion.p>
                <Reveal delay={0.1} className="mt-4">
                    <p className="ds-serif text-2xl italic text-primary">{job.org}</p>
                </Reveal>
            </div>

            {/* ---------- the story ---------- */}
            <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-8">
                <Reveal>
                    <h3 className="text-3xl font-semibold leading-[1.05] tracking-tight sm:text-4xl lg:text-5xl">{job.role}</h3>
                </Reveal>
                <Reveal delay={0.05}>
                    <p className="max-w-[60ch] text-base leading-7 text-muted-foreground sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">{job.summary}</p>
                </Reveal>

                <div className="relative">
                    <span aria-hidden className="absolute bottom-2 left-5 top-2 w-px -translate-x-1/2 bg-border" />
                    <motion.span
                        aria-hidden
                        style={reduce ? { scaleY: 1 } : { scaleY: rail }}
                        className="absolute bottom-2 left-5 top-2 w-px origin-top -translate-x-1/2 bg-primary"
                    />
                    <ul ref={list} className="flex flex-col gap-6">
                        {job.points.map((p, i) => (
                            <Point key={p} text={p} i={i} n={job.points.length} progress={rail} reduce={reduce} />
                        ))}
                    </ul>
                </div>

                <Reveal>
                    <ul className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-2 font-mono text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                        <li className="text-foreground">Built with</li>
                        {job.stack.map((s, i) => (
                            <li key={s} className="flex items-center gap-3">
                                {i > 0 && <span aria-hidden>/</span>}
                                <span className="ed-link cursor-default hover:text-foreground">{s}</span>
                            </li>
                        ))}
                    </ul>
                </Reveal>
            </div>
        </article>
    );
}

export default function Experience() {
    const reduce = !!useReducedMotion();

    return (
        <section id="experience" className="relative border-t border-border bg-background px-4 py-20 sm:px-6 lg:py-28">
            <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-6xl flex-col gap-12 lg:gap-16">
                <SectionHeader index="04" label={copy.badge} top={copy.headingTop} bottom={copy.headingBottom} reduce={reduce} />

                <div className="flex flex-col gap-16">
                    {experience.map((job, i) => (
                        <Chapter key={job.role + job.org} job={job} index={i} total={experience.length} reduce={reduce} />
                    ))}
                </div>

                {/* ---------- margin notes ---------- */}
                <div className="grid gap-10 border-t border-border pt-10 md:grid-cols-2 md:gap-14">
                    <Reveal>
                        <p className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">Education · {education.year}</p>
                        <h3 className="ds-serif mt-3 text-3xl italic leading-tight sm:text-4xl">{education.degree}</h3>
                        <p className="mt-3 text-base text-muted-foreground">{education.school}</p>
                    </Reveal>

                    <Reveal delay={0.1}>
                        <p className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">Writing</p>
                        <ul className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-5">
                            {writing.map((w) => (
                                <li key={w.href}>
                                    <a
                                        href={w.href}
                                        target="_blank"
                                        rel="noreferrer noopener"
                                        className="group inline-flex flex-col gap-1 rounded-sm outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary"
                                    >
                                        <span className="ed-link text-xl font-semibold leading-snug tracking-tight sm:text-2xl">{w.title}</span>
                                        <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                                            {w.source} <span aria-hidden className="inline-block transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:-translate-y-0.5 group-hover:translate-x-0.5">↗</span>
                                        </span>
                                    </a>
                                </li>
                            ))}
                        </ul>
                    </Reveal>
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>
    );
}

FILEPATH: ./src/components/section/footer/Footer.tsx

"use client";
// redesign.sh:v1

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type MouseEvent } from "react";
import { useReducedMotion, useScroll } from "framer-motion";
import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
import Reveal from "@/components/motion/Reveal";
import ScrollChars from "@/components/motion/ScrollChars";
import { site } from "@/content/site";
import { useMinWidth } from "@/hooks/useMinWidth";

const PAGES = site.nav;
const ELSEWHERE = [...site.social, { label: "Email", href: `mailto:${site.email}` }];

function FooterLink({ label, href, reduce }: { label: string; href: string; reduce: boolean }) {
    const external = href.startsWith("http");
    function onClick(e: MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) {
        if (!href.startsWith("#")) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        document.querySelector(href)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: reduce ? "auto" : "smooth" });
    }
    return (
        <a
            href={href}
            onClick={onClick}
            target={external ? "_blank" : undefined}
            rel={external ? "noreferrer noopener" : undefined}
            className="ed-link rounded-sm text-base outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary"
        >
            {label}
            {external && <span aria-hidden> ↗</span>}
        </a>
    );
}

/** Local time, computed on the client so it never mismatches on hydration. */
function LocalClock() {
    const [time, setTime] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        const fmt = () =>
            new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-IN", {
                hour: "2-digit",
                minute: "2-digit",
                hour12: false,
                timeZone: site.timezone,
            }).format(new Date());
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
        setTime(fmt());
        const id = window.setInterval(() => setTime(fmt()), 15000);
        return () => window.clearInterval(id);
    }, []);

    return (
        <p className="font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground" suppressHydrationWarning>
            {site.location} · <span className="tabular-nums">{time ?? "--:--"}</span> IST
        </p>
    );
}

export default function Footer() {
    const reduce = !!useReducedMotion();
    const desktop = useMinWidth(768);
    const ref = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
    const year = new Date().getFullYear();

    // 0 when the footer's top meets the viewport bottom, 1 when the page ends.
    const { scrollYProgress } = useScroll({ target: ref, offset: ["start end", "end end"] });
    const live = !reduce;

    function toTop() {
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: reduce ? "auto" : "smooth" });
    }

    return (
        <footer
            id="footer"
            ref={ref}
            className="relative flex min-h-[90svh] w-full max-w-[100vw] flex-col justify-between gap-16 overflow-x-clip border-t border-border bg-background px-4 pb-6 pt-24 sm:px-6"
        >
            <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-6xl flex-col gap-14">
                <Reveal className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2.5 text-sm font-medium">
                        <span className="relative flex size-2">
                            <span className="absolute inline-flex size-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-primary opacity-60 motion-reduce:animate-none" />
                            <span className="relative inline-flex size-2 rounded-full bg-primary" />
                        </span>
                        Open to new projects
                    </span>
                    <LocalClock />
                </Reveal>

                <div className="grid gap-12 md:grid-cols-[minmax(0,6fr)_minmax(0,3fr)_minmax(0,3fr)] md:gap-10">
                    <Reveal>
                        <p className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">Say hello</p>
                        <a
                            href={`mailto:${site.email}`}
                            className="ed-link ds-serif mt-3 inline-block rounded-sm text-[clamp(1.6rem,3.6vw,3.25rem)] italic leading-tight outline-none [overflow-wrap:anywhere] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary"
                        >
                            {site.email}
                        </a>
                    </Reveal>

                    <Reveal delay={0.08}>
                        <nav aria-label="Pages">
                            <p className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">Pages</p>
                            <ul className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2.5">
                                {PAGES.map((p) => (
                                    <li key={p.label}>
                                        <FooterLink label={p.label} href={p.href} reduce={reduce} />
                                    </li>
                                ))}
                            </ul>
                        </nav>
                    </Reveal>

                    <Reveal delay={0.16}>
                        <p className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">Find me</p>
                        <ul className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2.5">
                            {ELSEWHERE.map((l) => (
                                <li key={l.label}>
                                    <FooterLink label={l.label} href={l.href} reduce={reduce} />
                                </li>
                            ))}
                        </ul>
                    </Reveal>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-[1600px] flex-col gap-6">
                {/* The signature: one character at a time, driven by scroll. */}
                <h2
                    aria-label={site.fullName}
                    className="flex flex-col gap-1 font-semibold uppercase leading-none tracking-tighter text-[length:min(20vw,9rem)] transition-none md:flex-row md:justify-between md:gap-6 md:text-[length:min(11.2vw,12rem)]"
                >
                    <ScrollChars text="RAUNAK" progress={scrollYProgress} from={0.3} to={desktop ? 0.66 : 0.62} live={live} charClassName="transition-colors duration-300 hover:text-primary" />
                    <ScrollChars text="YADAV" progress={scrollYProgress} from={desktop ? 0.62 : 0.6} to={0.98} live={live} charClassName="transition-colors duration-300 hover:text-primary" />
                </h2>

                <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-t border-border pt-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        © {year} {site.fullName}. Designed and built by hand.
                    </p>
                    <Button
                        variant="outline"
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={toTop}
                        icon={
                            <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden className="size-4 transition-transform duration-300 group-hover/btn:-translate-y-0.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
                                <path d="M12 19V5M5 12l7-7 7 7" />
                            </svg>
                        }
                    >
                        Back to top
                    </Button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </footer>
    );
}

FILEPATH: ./src/components/section/hero/Hero.tsx
========================================================================
"use client";
/* redesign:v1 */

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type CSSProperties } from "react";
import {
    AnimatePresence,
    motion,
    useReducedMotion,
    type Variants,
} from "framer-motion";
import { HugeiconsIcon } from "@hugeicons/react";
import { ArrowDown01Icon } from "@hugeicons/core-free-icons";
import HeroTray from "@/components/ui/Herotray";
import NightSky from "@/components/hero/NightSky";
import Eyebrow from "@/components/ui/Eyebrow";
import PlayButton from "@/components/ui/PlayButton";
import { useScroll, useTransform } from "framer-motion";
import { TINT_LIST as TINTS } from "@/lib/tokens";

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  Shared with the Skills section                                            */
/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

const bouncy = { type: "spring", stiffness: 320, damping: 16 } as const;

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  Name: letters rise in, then each one jumps and takes a color on hover     */
/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

const letter: Variants = {
    hidden: { y: "110%", opacity: 0, rotate: 4, filter: "blur(8px)" },
    show: {
        y: "0%",
        opacity: 1,
        rotate: 0,
        filter: "blur(0px)",
        transition: { duration: 0.9, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] },
    },
};

function Char({
    char,
    i,
    settled,
    accent,
}: {
    char: string;
    i: number;
    settled: boolean;
    accent?: boolean;
}) {
    const tint = TINTS[i % TINTS.length];
    return (
        <span
            className={`mb-[-0.12em] inline-block pb-[0.12em] ${
                settled ? "" : "overflow-hidden"
            }`}
        >
            <motion.span
                variants={letter}
                whileHover={
                    settled
                        ? {
                              y: "-14%",
                              rotate: i % 2 ? 7 : -7,
                              color: tint,
                              transition: bouncy,
                          }
                        : undefined
                }
                className={`inline-block cursor-default ${accent ? "text-primary" : ""}`}
            >
                {char}
            </motion.span>
        </span>
    );
}

function AnimatedName({ text }: { text: string }) {
    const words = text.split(" ");
    const [settled, setSettled] = useState(false);

    // let the intro finish, then unclip so the letters can jump
    useEffect(() => {
        const t = window.setTimeout(() => setSettled(true), 1900);
        return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
    }, []);

    let n = 0;
    return (
        <motion.h1
            aria-label={text}
            initial="hidden"
            animate="show"
            transition={{ staggerChildren: 0.045, delayChildren: 0.35 }}
            className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-x-[0.28em] text-5xl font-semibold leading-[1.05] tracking-tight min-[400px]:text-6xl sm:text-7xl md:text-8xl lg:text-[clamp(5rem,min(10vw,17vh),9rem)]"
        >
            {words.map((word, wi) => (
                <span key={wi} className="inline-flex" aria-hidden>
                    {word.split("").map((char) => (
                        <Char key={n} char={char} i={n++} settled={settled} />
                    ))}
                    {wi === words.length - 1 && (
                        <Char char="." i={n++} settled={settled} accent />
                    )}
                </span>
            ))}
        </motion.h1>
    );
}

const fadeUp = (delay: number) => ({
    initial: { opacity: 0, y: 18, filter: "blur(6px)" },
    animate: { opacity: 1, y: 0, filter: "blur(0px)" },
    transition: { duration: 0.8, delay, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] as const },
});

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  Rotating word, with the same marker swipe as the Skills heading           */
/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

const WORDS = ["products", "APIs", "interfaces", "systems"];

function Rotator({ reduce }: { reduce: boolean }) {
    const [i, setI] = useState(0);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (reduce) return;
        const t = window.setInterval(
            () => setI((v) => (v + 1) % WORDS.length),
            2200,
        );
        return () => window.clearInterval(t);
    }, [reduce]);

    const tint = TINTS[i % TINTS.length];
    const mark = `linear-gradient(color-mix(in oklab, ${tint} 40%, transparent), color-mix(in oklab, ${tint} 40%, transparent))`;

    return (
        <span
            className="inline-block overflow-hidden align-bottom text-foreground"
            style={{
                backgroundImage: mark,
                backgroundSize: "100% 38%",
                backgroundPosition: "0 88%",
                backgroundRepeat: "no-repeat",
                transition: "background-image 0.4s",
            }}
        >
            <AnimatePresence mode="wait" initial={false}>
                <motion.span
                    key={WORDS[i]}
                    initial={reduce ? false : { y: "100%", opacity: 0 }}
                    animate={{ y: "0%", opacity: 1 }}
                    exit={reduce ? undefined : { y: "-100%", opacity: 0 }}
                    transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 240, damping: 22 }}
                    className="inline-block"
                >
                    {WORDS[i]}
                </motion.span>
            </AnimatePresence>
        </span>
    );
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  Sticker chips: grab one, fling it, it snaps back                          */
/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

const STICKERS = [
    { label: "Next.js", tilt: -4 },
    { label: "TypeScript", tilt: 3 },
    { label: "AI agents", tilt: -2 },
    { label: "Spring Boot", tilt: 4 },
];

function Stickers({ reduce }: { reduce: boolean }) {
    return (
        <ul className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
            {STICKERS.map((s, i) => (
                <motion.li
                    key={s.label}
                    data-sticker
                    drag={!reduce}
                    dragSnapToOrigin
                    dragElastic={0.5}
                    dragMomentum={false}
                    initial={
                        reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, scale: 0.5, y: 20, rotate: -10 }
                    }
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0, rotate: s.tilt }}
                    transition={{
                        type: "spring",
                        stiffness: 260,
                        damping: 17,
                        delay: reduce ? 0 : 1.35 + i * 0.08,
                    }}
                    whileHover={reduce ? undefined : { y: -4, scale: 1.06 }}
                    whileTap={reduce ? undefined : { scale: 0.95 }}
                    whileDrag={{ scale: 1.1, zIndex: 20 }}
                    style={{ "--tint": TINTS[i % TINTS.length] } as CSSProperties}
                    className="inline-flex cursor-grab touch-none select-none items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_40%,transparent)] bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_10%,var(--background))] px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium active:cursor-grabbing sm:text-sm"
                >
                    <span className="size-1.5 rounded-full bg-[color:var(--tint)]" />
                    {s.label}
                </motion.li>
            ))}
        </ul>
    );
}
