FILEPATH: ./src/components/section/navbar/AccentChanger.tsx
========================================================================
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { AnimatePresence, motion, type Variants } from "framer-motion";
import { ACCENTS, DEFAULT_ACCENT, applyAccent, readStoredAccent, storeAccent } from "@/lib/accent";
import { runViewTransition } from "@/lib/view-transition";

const panel: Variants = {
    hidden: { opacity: 0, y: -8, scale: 0.94, transition: { duration: 0.12 } },
    visible: {
        opacity: 1,
        y: 4,
        scale: 1,
        transition: { duration: 0.18, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1], staggerChildren: 0.035 },
    },
};

const bead: Variants = {
    hidden: { opacity: 0, scale: 0.4 },
    visible: { opacity: 1, scale: 1, transition: { type: "spring", stiffness: 420, damping: 22 } },
};

/**
 * Accent colour picker. The inline script in app/layout.tsx applies the saved
 * colour before first paint; this component only changes it.
 * This component mounts after hydration, so reading localStorage in the
 * initialiser is safe.
 */
export default function ThemeColorPicker() {
    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
    const [color, setColor] = useState<string>(() => readStoredAccent() ?? DEFAULT_ACCENT);
    const wrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const closeTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!open) return;
        const onDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {
            if (wrapRef.current && !wrapRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
        };
        const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
            if (e.key === "Escape") setOpen(false);
        };
        document.addEventListener("pointerdown", onDown);
        document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
        return () => {
            document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", onDown);
            document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
        };
    }, [open]);

    useEffect(
        () => () => {
            if (closeTimer.current) clearTimeout(closeTimer.current);
        },
        [],
    );

    const clearTimer = () => {
        if (closeTimer.current) {
            clearTimeout(closeTimer.current);
            closeTimer.current = null;
        }
    };

    const select = useCallback(
        (hex: string, el: HTMLElement) => {
            setOpen(false);
            if (hex === color) return;
            const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
            runViewTransition(
                () => {
                    applyAccent(hex);
                    storeAccent(hex);
                    setColor(hex);
                },
                { x: r.left + r.width / 2, y: r.top + r.height / 2 },
                450,
            );
        },
        [color],
    );

    const current = ACCENTS.find((a) => a.value === color)?.name ?? "Custom";

    return (
        <div
            ref={wrapRef}
            className="relative flex flex-col items-center"
            onPointerEnter={(e) => {
                if (e.pointerType !== "mouse") return;
                clearTimer();
                setOpen(true);
            }}
            onPointerLeave={(e) => {
                if (e.pointerType !== "mouse") return;
                clearTimer();
                closeTimer.current = setTimeout(() => setOpen(false), 140);
            }}
        >
            <button
                type="button"
                aria-label={`Accent colour: ${current}. Change`}
                aria-haspopup="true"
                aria-expanded={open}
                onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
                className="grid size-11 place-items-center rounded-full outline-none transition-transform hover:scale-105 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary/60 active:scale-95"
            >
                <span className="size-5 rounded-full bg-primary ring-2 ring-background ring-offset-1 ring-offset-primary/30" />
            </button>

            <AnimatePresence>
                {open && (
                    <motion.div
                        key="accent-panel"
                        role="group"
                        aria-label="Accent colour options"
                        variants={panel}
                        initial="hidden"
                        animate="visible"
                        exit="hidden"
                        className="absolute left-1/2 top-full z-[1001] mt-1 flex -translate-x-1/2 flex-col gap-0.5 rounded-full border border-border bg-background/85 p-1 shadow-xl backdrop-blur-xl"
                    >
                        {ACCENTS.map((c) => (
                            <motion.button
                                key={c.value}
                                type="button"
                                variants={bead}
                                aria-label={c.name}
                                aria-pressed={c.value === color}
                                title={c.name}
                                onClick={(e) => select(c.value, e.currentTarget)}
                                className="grid size-9 place-items-center rounded-full outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary"
                            >
                                <span
                                    className={`size-5 rounded-full transition-transform hover:scale-125 ${
                                        c.value === color ? "ring-2 ring-foreground ring-offset-2 ring-offset-background" : ""
                                    }`}
                                    style={{ backgroundColor: c.value }}
                                />
                            </motion.button>
                        ))}
                    </motion.div>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
    );
}


========================================================================
FILEPATH: ./src/components/section/navbar/Navbar.tsx

// Navbar.tsx
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import {
    AnimatePresence,
    motion,
    useMotionValue,
    useMotionValueEvent,
    useReducedMotion,
    useScroll,
    useSpring,
    useTransform,
    type Transition,
    type Variants,
} from "framer-motion";
import { HugeiconsIcon } from "@hugeicons/react";
import {
    Home01Icon,
    UserIcon,
    CodeIcon,
    Folder01Icon,
    Mail01Icon,
    Menu01Icon,
    Briefcase01Icon,
    Cancel01Icon,
} from "@hugeicons/core-free-icons";

import { ThemeToggle } from "../../ThemeToggle";
import ThemeColorDropdown from "./AccentChanger";
import Data from "@/Data/Data.json";

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Config
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const NAV_ITEMS = [
    { id: "home", label: "Home", icon: Home01Icon },
    { id: "about", label: "About", icon: UserIcon },
    { id: "skills", label: "Skills", icon: CodeIcon },
    { id: "projects", label: "Projects", icon: Folder01Icon },
    { id: "experience", label: "Experience", icon: Briefcase01Icon },
    { id: "contact", label: "Contact", icon: Mail01Icon },
] as const;

type NavId = (typeof NAV_ITEMS)[number]["id"];

const MOBILE_ITEMS = NAV_ITEMS.filter((i) => i.id !== "home");

const NAV_HEIGHT = 48; // px — radius is 50% of this (full pill)
const COMPACT_WIDTH = 124; // px — collapsed "Raunak." pill
const EXPANDED_MAX = 880; // px
const EXPANDED_VW = 0.94;
const SCROLL_THRESHOLD = 80; // px before collapsing
const DOWN_DEADZONE = 6; // px of downward scroll to count
const UP_DEADZONE = 10; // px of upward scroll to count (avoids accidental expands)
const HOVER_SCROLL_GUARD_MS = 350; // ignore hover right after scrolling
const LEAVE_DELAY_MS = 180; // grace period before collapsing after mouse leaves

// One spring drives the whole collapse/expand (critically damped = no wobble).
const T_SPRING = { stiffness: 210, damping: 30, mass: 0.9, restDelta: 0.0005 };
const T_SPRING_REDUCED = { stiffness: 4000, damping: 200, mass: 1 };

// Snappier spring for the sliding link highlight and menu.
const UI_SPRING: Transition = {
    type: "spring",
    stiffness: 380,
    damping: 32,
    mass: 0.8,
};

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Hooks
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Tracks which section is under the middle of the viewport. */
function useActiveSection(): NavId {
    const [active, setActive] = useState<NavId>("home");

    useEffect(() => {
        const els = NAV_ITEMS.map((i) => document.getElementById(i.id)).filter(
            (el): el is HTMLElement => el !== null
        );

        const io = new IntersectionObserver(
            (entries) => {
                entries.forEach((entry) => {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) setActive(entry.target.id as NavId);
                });
            },
            { rootMargin: "-45% 0px -50% 0px" }
        );
        els.forEach((el) => io.observe(el));

        const onScroll = () => {
            if (window.scrollY < 120) setActive("home");
        };
        window.addEventListener("scroll", onScroll, { passive: true });
        onScroll();

        return () => {
            io.disconnect();
            window.removeEventListener("scroll", onScroll);
        };
    }, []);

    return active;
}

/** Expanded pill width in px, kept in sync with the viewport. */
function useExpandedWidth(): number {
    const [width, setWidth] = useState(EXPANDED_MAX);

    useEffect(() => {
        let raf = 0;
        const compute = () =>
            setWidth(Math.min(window.innerWidth * EXPANDED_VW, EXPANDED_MAX));
        const onResize = () => {
            cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
            raf = requestAnimationFrame(compute);
        };
        compute();
        window.addEventListener("resize", onResize, { passive: true });
        return () => {
            window.removeEventListener("resize", onResize);
            cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
        };
    }, []);

    return width;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Mobile menu
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const menuVariants: Variants = {
    hidden: { opacity: 0, y: -10, scale: 0.96 },
    visible: {
        opacity: 1,
        y: 0,
        scale: 1,
        transition: { ...UI_SPRING, staggerChildren: 0.045, delayChildren: 0.04 },
    },
    exit: {
        opacity: 0,
        y: -8,
        scale: 0.97,
        transition: { duration: 0.14, ease: "easeIn" },
    },
};

const itemVariants: Variants = {
    hidden: { opacity: 0, x: -10 },
    visible: { opacity: 1, x: 0, transition: UI_SPRING },
    exit: { opacity: 0, transition: { duration: 0.08 } },
};

interface MobileMenuProps {
    open: boolean;
    width: number;
    active: NavId;
    onNavigate: (e: React.MouseEvent, id: NavId) => void;
}

function MobileMenu({ open, width, active, onNavigate }: MobileMenuProps) {
    return (
        <AnimatePresence>
            {open && (
                <motion.div
                    key="mobile-menu"
                    variants={menuVariants}
                    initial="hidden"
                    animate="visible"
                    exit="exit"
                    style={{ width, transformOrigin: "top center" }}
                    className="pointer-events-auto mt-2 flex flex-col gap-1 rounded-[28px] border border-border bg-background/85 p-2 shadow-xl backdrop-blur-xl"
                >
                    {MOBILE_ITEMS.map((item) => {
                        const isActive = active === item.id;
                        return (
                            <motion.div key={item.id} variants={itemVariants}>
                                <Link
                                    href={`/#${item.id}`}
                                    onClick={(e) => onNavigate(e, item.id)}
                                    aria-current={isActive ? "true" : undefined}
                                    className={`flex items-center gap-3 rounded-full px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium transition-colors duration-150 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary/60 ${
                                        isActive
                                            ? "bg-primary/10 text-foreground"
                                            : "text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground"
                                    }`}
                                >
                                    <HugeiconsIcon
                                        icon={item.icon}
                                        size={18}
                                        strokeWidth={1.75}
                                        className={isActive ? "text-primary" : ""}
                                    />
                                    <span className="flex-1">{item.label}</span>
                                    {isActive && (
                                        <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-primary" />
                                    )}
                                </Link>
                            </motion.div>
                        );
                    })}
                </motion.div>
            )}
        </AnimatePresence>
    );
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Navbar
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export default function Navbar() {
    const pathname = usePathname();
    const reduceMotion = useReducedMotion();

    const navRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
    const lastY = useRef(0);
    const lastScrollAt = useRef(0);
    const leaveTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

    const [entered, setEntered] = useState(false);
    const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(
        () => typeof window !== "undefined" && window.scrollY > SCROLL_THRESHOLD
    );
    const [peek, setPeek] = useState(false); // hover / focus / tap re-expands
    const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
    const [hovered, setHovered] = useState<NavId | null>(null);

    const active = useActiveSection();
    const expandedWidth = useExpandedWidth();

    const expanded = entered && (!collapsed || peek || menuOpen);
    const highlighted = hovered ?? active;

    const firstName = Data.hero.firstName as string;

    // ── Single spring drives everything: 0 = compact, 1 = expanded ──────────
    const t = useSpring(0, reduceMotion ? T_SPRING_REDUCED : T_SPRING);
    const expandedMV = useMotionValue(EXPANDED_MAX);

    useEffect(() => {
        expandedMV.set(expandedWidth);
    }, [expandedWidth, expandedMV]);

    useEffect(() => {
        t.set(expanded ? 1 : 0);
    }, [expanded, t]);

    // Derived values — computed per frame outside React.
    const pillWidth = useTransform([t, expandedMV], (latest: number[]) => {
        const [tv, ev] = latest;
        return COMPACT_WIDTH + (ev - COMPACT_WIDTH) * Math.min(Math.max(tv, 0), 1);
    });
    const innerWidth = useTransform(expandedMV, (v) => v - 2); // minus 1px borders

    const compactOpacity = useTransform(t, [0.1, 0.5], [1, 0]);
    const compactScale = useTransform(t, [0, 0.5], [1, 0.9]);
    const compactVisibility = useTransform(t, (v) => (v > 0.5 ? "hidden" : "visible"));

    const fullOpacity = useTransform(t, [0.5, 0.9], [0, 1]);
    const fullScale = useTransform(t, [0.5, 1], [0.96, 1]);
    const fullVisibility = useTransform(t, (v) => (v < 0.5 ? "hidden" : "visible"));

    // Clip the full content to the pill's current shape while animating,
    // then remove the clip entirely at rest so dropdowns are never cut off.
    const fullClip = useTransform([t, expandedMV], (latest: number[]) => {
        const [tv, ev] = latest;
        if (tv >= 0.999) return "none";
        const w = COMPACT_WIDTH + (ev - COMPACT_WIDTH) * Math.max(0, tv);
        const inset = Math.max(0, (ev - w) / 2);
        return `inset(0px ${inset}px round ${NAV_HEIGHT / 2}px)`;
    });

    // ── Entry: bead pops in, then expands ───────────────────────────────────
    useEffect(() => {
        lastY.current = window.scrollY;
        const id = setTimeout(() => setEntered(true), 220);
        return () => clearTimeout(id);
    }, []);

    // ── Scroll progress line ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const { scrollY, scrollYProgress } = useScroll();
    const progress = useSpring(scrollYProgress, {
        stiffness: 120,
        damping: 30,
        restDelta: 0.001,
    });

    // ── Collapse on scroll down, expand on scroll up ────────────────────────
    useMotionValueEvent(scrollY, "change", (y) => {
        lastScrollAt.current = performance.now();

        // Ignore rubber-band overscroll at the very top / bottom.
        const max = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight;
        if (y < 0 || y > max) return;

        const delta = y - lastY.current;

        if (y <= SCROLL_THRESHOLD) {
            lastY.current = y;
            setCollapsed(false);
            return;
        }
        if (delta > DOWN_DEADZONE) {
            lastY.current = y;
            setCollapsed(true);
            setPeek(false);
            setMenuOpen(false);
        } else if (delta < -UP_DEADZONE) {
            lastY.current = y;
            setCollapsed(false);
        }
    });

    // ── Outside click closes menu / peek, Escape closes menu ────────────────
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!menuOpen && !peek) return;
        const onPointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {
            if (navRef.current && !navRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
                setMenuOpen(false);
                setPeek(false);
            }
        };
        const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
            if (e.key === "Escape") setMenuOpen(false);
        };
        document.addEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown);
        document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
        return () => {
            document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown);
            document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
        };
    }, [menuOpen, peek]);

    useEffect(
        () => () => {
            if (leaveTimer.current) clearTimeout(leaveTimer.current);
        },
        []
    );

    // ── Hover handling (mouse only, ignores synthetic post-scroll events) ───
    const clearLeaveTimer = () => {
        if (leaveTimer.current) {
            clearTimeout(leaveTimer.current);
            leaveTimer.current = null;
        }
    };

    const onPointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
        if (e.pointerType !== "mouse") return;
        clearLeaveTimer();
        if (!collapsed || peek) return;
        if (performance.now() - lastScrollAt.current < HOVER_SCROLL_GUARD_MS) return;
        if (e.movementX === 0 && e.movementY === 0) return;
        setPeek(true);
    };

    const onPointerLeave = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
        if (e.pointerType !== "mouse") return;
        setHovered(null);
        clearLeaveTimer();
        leaveTimer.current = setTimeout(() => setPeek(false), LEAVE_DELAY_MS);
    };

    // ── Smooth in-page navigation ───────────────────────────────────────────
    const goTo = useCallback(
        (e: React.MouseEvent, id: NavId) => {
            setMenuOpen(false);
            if (pathname !== "/") return; // let Next route to "/#id"
            e.preventDefault();
            if (id === "home") {
                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
            } else {
                document
                    .getElementById(id)
                    ?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
            }
            window.history.replaceState(null, "", id === "home" ? "/" : `/#${id}`);
        },
        [pathname]
    );

    return (
        <nav
            ref={navRef}
            aria-label="Primary"
            className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-x-0 top-4 z-50 flex flex-col items-center px-3"
        >
            {/* ── Pill ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
            <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -14, scale: 0.9 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.45, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] }}
                onPointerMove={onPointerMove}
                onPointerLeave={onPointerLeave}
                onFocusCapture={() => collapsed && setPeek(true)}
                style={{
                    width: pillWidth,
                    height: NAV_HEIGHT,
                    borderRadius: NAV_HEIGHT / 2,
                    willChange: "width",
                }}
                className="pointer-events-auto relative border border-border bg-background/80 shadow-[0_8px_30px_rgb(0,0,0,0.08)] backdrop-blur-md dark:shadow-[0_8px_30px_rgb(0,0,0,0.45)]"
            >
                {/* Scroll progress — clipped to the pill's curve */}
                <div
                    className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden"
                    style={{ borderRadius: NAV_HEIGHT / 2 }}
                    aria-hidden="true"
                >
                    <motion.div
                        style={{ scaleX: progress }}
                        className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 h-[2px] w-full origin-left bg-primary/80"
                    />
                </div>

                {/* ── Compact: just the name ─────────────────────────────── */}
                <motion.div
                    role="button"
                    tabIndex={0}
                    aria-label="Expand navigation"
                    onClick={() => setPeek(true)}
                    onKeyDown={(e) => {
                        if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") setPeek(true);
                    }}
                    style={{
                        opacity: compactOpacity,
                        scale: compactScale,
                        visibility: compactVisibility,
                        borderRadius: NAV_HEIGHT / 2,
                    }}
                    className="absolute inset-0 flex cursor-pointer items-center justify-center focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary/60"
                >
                    <span className="text-[15px] font-semibold leading-none tracking-tight">
                        {firstName}
                        <span className="text-primary">.</span>
                    </span>
                </motion.div>

                {/* ── Full: fixed width, centered, never reflows ─────────── */}
                <motion.div
                    style={{
                        width: innerWidth,
                        left: "50%",
                        x: "-50%",
                        opacity: fullOpacity,
                        scale: fullScale,
                        visibility: fullVisibility,
                        clipPath: fullClip,
                    }}
                    className="absolute top-0 flex h-full items-center justify-between gap-3 pl-5 pr-2"
                >
                    {/* Logo */}
                    <Link
                        href="/"
                        onClick={(e) => goTo(e, "home")}
                        aria-label="Back to top"
                        className="shrink-0 rounded-full text-lg font-semibold leading-none tracking-tight focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary/60"
                    >
                        {firstName}
                        <span className="text-primary">.</span>
                    </Link>

                    {/* Desktop links */}
                    <div className="hidden items-center gap-0.5 lg:flex">
                        {NAV_ITEMS.map((item) => {
                            const isActive = active === item.id;
                            const isHighlighted = highlighted === item.id;
                            return (
                                <Link
                                    key={item.id}
                                    href={`/#${item.id}`}
                                    onClick={(e) => goTo(e, item.id)}
                                    onPointerEnter={(e) =>
                                        e.pointerType === "mouse" && setHovered(item.id)
                                    }
                                    onFocus={() => setHovered(item.id)}
                                    onBlur={() => setHovered(null)}
                                    aria-current={isActive ? "true" : undefined}
                                    className={`relative flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full px-3 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors duration-150 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary/60 ${
                                        isHighlighted
                                            ? "text-foreground"
                                            : "text-muted-foreground"
                                    }`}
                                >
                                    {isHighlighted && (
                                        <motion.span
                                            layoutId="nav-highlight"
                                            transition={UI_SPRING}
                                            className={`absolute inset-0 rounded-full ${
                                                hovered && hovered !== active
                                                    ? "bg-muted"
                                                    : "bg-primary/10"
                                            }`}
                                        />
                                    )}
                                    <HugeiconsIcon
                                        icon={item.icon}
                                        size={15}
                                        strokeWidth={1.75}
                                        className={`relative z-10 transition-colors duration-150 ${
                                            isActive ? "text-primary" : ""
                                        }`}
                                    />
                                    <span className="relative z-10">{item.label}</span>
                                </Link>
                            );
                        })}
                    </div>

                    {/* Right-side controls */}
                    <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 pr-3">
                        <ThemeToggle />
                        <ThemeColorDropdown />
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setMenuOpen((o) => !o)}
                            aria-label={menuOpen ? "Close menu" : "Open menu"}
                            aria-expanded={menuOpen}
                            className="grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-full text-muted-foreground transition-colors duration-150 hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary/60 lg:hidden"
                        >
                            <AnimatePresence mode="wait" initial={false}>
                                <motion.span
                                    key={menuOpen ? "close" : "open"}
                                    initial={{ rotate: -90, opacity: 0, scale: 0.7 }}
                                    animate={{ rotate: 0, opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
                                    exit={{ rotate: 90, opacity: 0, scale: 0.7 }}
                                    transition={{ duration: 0.16 }}
                                    className="grid place-items-center"
                                >
                                    <HugeiconsIcon
                                        icon={menuOpen ? Cancel01Icon : Menu01Icon}
                                        size={18}
                                        strokeWidth={1.75}
                                    />
                                </motion.span>
                            </AnimatePresence>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </motion.div>
            </motion.div>

            {/* ── Mobile dropdown — sibling of the pill ──────────────────── */}
            <MobileMenu
                open={menuOpen}
                width={expandedWidth}
                active={active}
                onNavigate={goTo}
            />
        </nav>
    );
}

"use client";
// redesign.sh:v1

import { useEffect, useRef, type CSSProperties } from "react";
import { animate, motion, useInView, useMotionValue, useTransform } from "framer-motion";
import { STATUS, type LinkKind, type Metric, type StatusKind } from "@/content/projects";

export function GithubMark({ className }: { className?: string }) {
    return (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden className={className} fill="currentColor">
            <path d="M12 .5a11.5 11.5 0 0 0-3.64 22.41c.58.1.79-.25.79-.56v-2c-3.2.7-3.88-1.37-3.88-1.37-.52-1.33-1.28-1.69-1.28-1.69-1.04-.71.08-.7.08-.7 1.15.08 1.76 1.18 1.76 1.18 1.03 1.76 2.7 1.25 3.36.96.1-.75.4-1.25.73-1.54-2.55-.29-5.24-1.28-5.24-5.69 0-1.26.45-2.28 1.18-3.09-.12-.29-.51-1.46.11-3.05 0 0 .97-.31 3.17 1.18a11 11 0 0 1 5.78 0c2.2-1.49 3.17-1.18 3.17-1.18.63 1.59.23 2.76.11 3.05.74.81 1.18 1.83 1.18 3.09 0 4.42-2.69 5.39-5.25 5.68.41.36.78 1.06.78 2.14v3.17c0 .31.21.67.8.56A11.5 11.5 0 0 0 12 .5Z" />
        </svg>
    );
}

function Stroke({ className, children }: { className?: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
    return (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden className={className} fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
            {children}
        </svg>
    );
}

export function LinkIcon({ kind, className }: { kind: LinkKind; className?: string }) {
    if (kind === "repo") return <GithubMark className={className} />;
    if (kind === "post")
        return (
            <Stroke className={className}>
                <path d="M6 3h9l4 4v14H6z" />
                <path d="M14 3v5h5" />
                <path d="M9 13h7M9 17h5" />
            </Stroke>
        );
    return (
        <Stroke className={className}>
            <path d="M7 17 17 7" />
            <path d="M8 7h9v9" />
        </Stroke>
    );
}

export function Sparkle({ className }: { className?: string }) {
    return (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden className={className} fill="currentColor">
            <path d="M12 2c.6 4.8 2.2 7.4 10 10-7.8 2.6-9.4 5.2-10 10-.6-4.8-2.2-7.4-10-10 7.8-2.6 9.4-5.2 10-10Z" />
        </svg>
    );
}

export function StatusChip({ status }: { status: StatusKind }) {
    const s = STATUS[status];
    return (
        <span
            style={{ "--chip": s.color } as CSSProperties}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-muted-foreground"
        >
            <span className="relative flex size-1.5">
                <span className="absolute inline-flex size-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-[color:var(--chip)] opacity-60 motion-reduce:animate-none" />
                <span className="relative inline-flex size-1.5 rounded-full bg-[color:var(--chip)]" />
            </span>
            {s.label}
        </span>
    );
}

function CountUp({ to, suffix, reduce }: { to: number; suffix?: string; reduce: boolean }) {
    const ref = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
    const inView = useInView(ref, { once: true, amount: 0.6 });
    const mv = useMotionValue(reduce ? to : 0);
    const text = useTransform(mv, (v) => Math.round(v).toString());

    useEffect(() => {
        if (reduce) {
            mv.set(to);
            return;
        }
        if (!inView) return;
        const controls = animate(mv, to, { duration: 1.1, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] });
        return () => controls.stop();
    }, [to, reduce, inView, mv]);

    return (
        <span ref={ref} className="tabular-nums">
            <motion.span>{text}</motion.span>
            {suffix}
        </span>
    );
}

export function Metrics({ metrics, reduce }: { metrics: Metric[]; reduce: boolean }) {
    return (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 border-y border-border py-3">
            {metrics.map((m) => (
                <div key={m.label} className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                    <span className="text-lg font-semibold leading-none tracking-tight text-[color:var(--tint)]">
                        {typeof m.value === "number" ? <CountUp to={m.value} suffix={m.suffix} reduce={reduce} /> : m.value}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs leading-4 text-muted-foreground">{m.label}</span>
                </div>
            ))}
        </div>
    );
}

"use client";
// redesign.sh:v1

import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { HugeiconsIcon } from "@hugeicons/react";
import { ArrowRight01Icon } from "@hugeicons/core-free-icons";
import PlayButton from "@/components/ui/PlayButton";
import SectionHeader from "@/components/ui/SectionHeader";
import { copy, PROJECTS } from "@/content/projects";
import { useFinePointer } from "@/hooks/useFinePointer";
import { spring } from "@/lib/motion";
import { GithubMark } from "./ProjectParts";
import ProjectStage from "./ProjectStage";

/*
 * Sticky-stack Projects.
 * Desktop (lg+): every project is a full-height stage that sticks while the next
 * one rises over it (see the geometry note in ProjectStage.tsx).
 * Below lg: ordinary stacked panels, no sticky behaviour.
 *
 * IMPORTANT: no ancestor of a stage may set overflow other than visible/clip,
 * or position: sticky silently stops working. `isolate` keeps the stage
 * z-indexes inside this section.
 */

function ScrollHint({ text, reduce }: { text: string; reduce: boolean }) {
    return (
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-full border border-border py-1.5 pl-3 pr-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            <motion.span
                aria-hidden
                animate={reduce ? undefined : { y: [0, 4, 0] }}
                transition={{ duration: 1.5, repeat: Infinity, ease: "easeInOut" }}
                className="flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-primary"
            >
                <HugeiconsIcon icon={ArrowRight01Icon} size={14} strokeWidth={2} className="rotate-90" />
            </motion.span>
            <span className="truncate">{text}</span>
        </div>
    );
}

export default function Projects() {
    const reduce = !!useReducedMotion();
    const fine = useFinePointer();
    const hint = fine ? copy.hintFine : copy.hintTouch;

    return (
        <section id="projects" className="relative isolate box-border w-full overflow-x-clip border-t border-border bg-background pt-12 sm:pt-16">
            <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 sm:px-6">
                <SectionHeader
                    index="03"
                    label={copy.badge}
                    top={copy.headingTop}
                    bottom={copy.headingBottom}
                    reduce={reduce}
                    aside={<ScrollHint text={hint} reduce={reduce} />}
                />
            </div>

            <ol className="mt-10 flex list-none flex-col gap-10 px-3 sm:px-4 lg:mt-14 lg:gap-0 lg:px-0">
                {PROJECTS.map((project, i) => (
                    <ProjectStage key={project.id} project={project} index={i} last={i === PROJECTS.length - 1} reduce={reduce} />
                ))}
            </ol>

            <div className="relative z-[60] mx-auto max-w-6xl bg-background px-4 pb-20 pt-10 sm:px-6 lg:pb-28">
                <motion.div
                    initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 24 }}
                    whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                    viewport={{ once: true, amount: 0.5 }}
                    transition={spring}
                    className="flex flex-col items-start justify-between gap-4 border-t border-border pt-8 sm:flex-row sm:items-center"
                >
                    <div>
                        <p className="text-base font-semibold tracking-tight sm:text-lg">{copy.outroTitle}</p>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{copy.outroText}</p>
                    </div>
                    <PlayButton href={copy.githubProfile} variant="solid" external>
                        <GithubMark className="size-4" />
                        {copy.outroCta}
                    </PlayButton>
                </motion.div>
            </div>
        </section>
    );
}


========================================================================
FILEPATH: ./src/components/section/project/ProjectStage.tsx
========================================================================
"use client";
// redesign.sh:v1

import { useRef, type CSSProperties, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { motion, useScroll, useTransform, type MotionValue } from "framer-motion";
import PlayButton from "@/components/ui/PlayButton";
import ScrollChars from "@/components/motion/ScrollChars";
import { copy, TOTAL, type Project } from "@/content/projects";
import { useMinWidth } from "@/hooks/useMinWidth";
import { pad2 } from "@/lib/utils";
import { LinkIcon, Metrics, Sparkle, StatusChip } from "./ProjectParts";
import { visualFor } from "./visuals";

/*
 * STICKY STACK GEOMETRY (desktop, lg+)
 *
 *   <li>   relative, z-index = index + 1, margin-top: -100svh (except the first)
 *     <div>  height WRAP svh          <- scroll target
 *       <article> position: sticky; top: 0; height: 100svh
 *
 * Each stage sticks for (WRAP - 100)svh. The next stage's wrapper starts
 * (WRAP - 100)svh after this one, so it rises over the stuck stage while this
 * one keeps its place: a pure-CSS overlap. The numbers below only drive the
 * transforms inside the stage and must match the Tailwind classes.
 *
 *   WRAP = 250 -> "lg:h-[250svh]"      WRAP_LAST = 180 -> "lg:h-[180svh]"
 *   dwell (nothing moving) = WRAP - 200 = 50svh
 */
const WRAP = 250;
const WRAP_LAST = 180;

function Rise({
    enter,
    at,
    live,
    className = "",
    children,
}: {
    enter: MotionValue<number>;
    at: number;
    live: boolean;
    className?: string;
    children: ReactNode;
}) {
    const o = useTransform(enter, [at, at + 0.35], [0, 1]);
    const y = useTransform(enter, [at, at + 0.35], [28, 0]);
    return (
        <motion.div data-ed-hide style={live ? { opacity: o, y } : undefined} className={className}>
            {children}
        </motion.div>
    );
}

export default function ProjectStage({
    project,
    index,
    last,
    reduce,
}: {
    project: Project;
    index: number;
    last: boolean;
    reduce: boolean;
}) {
    const desktop = useMinWidth(1024);
    const live = desktop && !reduce;
    const wrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    const wrapH = last ? WRAP_LAST : WRAP;
    const L = 100 + wrapH;
    const enterEnd = 100 / L;
    const exitStart = (wrapH - 100) / L;
    const exitEnd = wrapH / L;

    const { scrollYProgress: p } = useScroll({ target: wrapRef, offset: ["start end", "end start"] });
    const enter = useTransform(p, [0, enterEnd], [0, 1]);
    const exit = useTransform(p, [exitStart, exitEnd], [0, 1]);

    const scale = useTransform(exit, [0, 1], [1, 0.95]);
    const lift = useTransform(exit, [0, 1], ["0%", "-3%"]);
    const dim = useTransform(exit, [0, 1], [0, 0.6]);
    const numY = useTransform(enter, [0, 1], ["22%", "0%"]);
    const numX = useTransform(exit, [0, 1], ["0%", "-8%"]);

    const Visual = visualFor(project.id);
    const covered = live && !last;
    const tintVars = { "--tint": project.tint, "--on-tint": "oklch(0.2 0 0)", zIndex: index + 1 } as CSSProperties;

    return (
        <li style={tintVars} className={`relative ${index > 0 ? "lg:-mt-[100svh]" : ""}`}>
            <div ref={wrapRef} className={last ? "lg:h-[180svh]" : "lg:h-[250svh]"}>
                <article aria-label={project.name} className="lg:sticky lg:top-0 lg:h-svh">
                    <motion.div
                        style={covered ? { scale, y: lift, transformOrigin: "50% 0%" } : undefined}
                        className="relative h-full overflow-hidden rounded-[1.25rem] border border-border bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_7%,var(--background))] lg:rounded-b-none lg:rounded-t-[2.25rem] lg:border-b-0"
                    >
                        <motion.span
                            aria-hidden
                            style={live ? { y: numY, x: covered ? numX : "0%" } : undefined}
                            className="ds-serif pointer-events-none absolute -right-2 -top-6 hidden select-none text-[clamp(14rem,30vw,32rem)] leading-none text-[color:var(--tint)] opacity-[0.08] lg:block"
                        >
                            {pad2(index + 1)}
                        </motion.span>

                        <div className="relative mx-auto grid h-full w-full max-w-7xl gap-8 px-5 py-8 sm:px-8 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,5fr)_minmax(0,7fr)] lg:items-center lg:gap-12 lg:py-0 lg:pt-8">
                            {/* ---------- words ---------- */}
                            <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-4 lg:gap-5">
                                <Rise enter={enter} at={0.02} live={live} className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 gap-y-1.5">
                                    <span className="font-mono text-xs tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">
                                        {pad2(index + 1)} / {pad2(TOTAL)}
                                    </span>
                                    <span className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted-foreground">{project.kind}</span>
                                    <StatusChip status={project.status} />
                                </Rise>

                                <h3 aria-label={project.name} className="ds-serif whitespace-nowrap text-[clamp(3rem,5.6vw,6.25rem)] font-normal leading-[0.95] tracking-tight">
                                    <ScrollChars text={project.name} progress={enter} from={0.02} to={0.75} live={live} />
                                </h3>

                                <Rise enter={enter} at={0.14} live={live}>
                                    <p className="max-w-[24ch] text-xl font-medium leading-snug tracking-tight lg:text-2xl">{project.cardLine}</p>
                                </Rise>

                                <Rise enter={enter} at={0.2} live={live}>
                                    <p className="max-w-[52ch] text-[15px] leading-7 text-muted-foreground">{project.summary}</p>
                                </Rise>

                                <Rise enter={enter} at={0.26} live={live}>
                                    <ul className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                                        {project.chips.map((c) => (
                                            <li key={c} className="inline-flex h-7 items-center rounded-full border border-border px-3 text-xs font-medium text-foreground/80">
                                                {c}
                                            </li>
                                        ))}
                                    </ul>
                                </Rise>

                                <Rise enter={enter} at={0.32} live={live} className="ed-tall-only">
                                    <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2.5">
                                        {project.bullets.map((b) => (
                                            <li key={b} className="flex items-start gap-3">
                                                <Sparkle className="mt-[5px] size-3.5 shrink-0 text-[color:var(--tint)]" />
                                                <span className="text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground">{b}</span>
                                            </li>
                                        ))}
                                    </ul>
                                </Rise>

                                <Rise enter={enter} at={0.38} live={live}>
                                    <Metrics metrics={project.metrics} reduce={reduce} />
                                </Rise>

                                <Rise enter={enter} at={0.44} live={live} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
                                    <PlayButton href={project.caseStudy} variant="solid">
                                        {copy.caseStudy}
                                    </PlayButton>
                                    {project.links.map((l) => (
                                        <PlayButton key={l.href} href={l.href} external>
                                            <LinkIcon kind={l.kind} className="size-4" />
                                            {l.label}
                                        </PlayButton>
                                    ))}
                                    {project.links.length === 0 && <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{copy.comingSoon}</span>}
                                </Rise>
                            </div>

                            {/* ---------- visual ---------- */}
                            <div className="min-w-0">
                                <Visual project={project} enter={enter} exit={exit} live={live} reduce={reduce} />
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <motion.span
                            aria-hidden
                            style={{ opacity: covered ? dim : 0 }}
                            className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 z-40 bg-background"
                        />
                    </motion.div>
                </article>
            </div>
        </li>
    );
}


========================================================================
FILEPATH: ./src/components/section/project/visuals.tsx
========================================================================
"use client";
// redesign.sh:v1
/* eslint-disable @next/next/no-img-element */

import { useRef, useState, type ComponentType, type ReactNode } from "react";
import {
    motion,
    useMotionTemplate,
    useMotionValue,
    useSpring,
    useTransform,
    type MotionValue,
} from "framer-motion";
import type { Project } from "@/content/projects";
import { IMAGE_META } from "@/content/image-meta.generated";
import { clamp01 } from "@/lib/utils";

export type VisualProps = {
    project: Project;
    /** 0 -> 1 while this stage rises into place */
    enter: MotionValue<number>;
    /** 0 -> 1 while the next stage covers this one */
    exit: MotionValue<number>;
    /** desktop + motion allowed: scroll-driven transforms apply */
    live: boolean;
    reduce: boolean;
};

const ratioOf = (src?: string) => {
    const m = src ? IMAGE_META[src] : undefined;
    return m ? m.w / m.h : 1.6;
};

const SHADOW = "shadow-[0_30px_70px_-34px_rgba(0,0,0,0.6)]";
const EASE = "ease-[cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1)]";

function Shot({ src, alt, eager, className = "" }: { src: string; alt: string; eager?: boolean; className?: string }) {
    const m = IMAGE_META[src];
    return (
        <img
            src={src}
            alt={alt}
            width={m?.w}
            height={m?.h}
            loading={eager ? "eager" : "lazy"}
            decoding="async"
            draggable={false}
            className={`block h-auto w-full ${className}`}
        />
    );
}

function Frame({ children, ratio, className = "" }: { children: ReactNode; ratio: number; className?: string }) {
    return (
        <span
            style={{ aspectRatio: ratio, containerType: "inline-size" }}
            className={`relative block w-full overflow-hidden rounded-[6px] border border-border bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_12%,var(--background))] ${SHADOW} ${className}`}
        >
            {children}
        </span>
    );
}

function Missing({ project }: { project: Project }) {
    return (
        <span className="grid aspect-[16/10] w-full place-items-center rounded-[6px] border border-dashed border-border text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            {project.name}: screenshot unavailable
        </span>
    );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Hermes: the screenshot splits into five shutters. They slide apart and
   open onto an outlined wordmark that travels behind them. No lock/key motifs.
   --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
export function HermesVisual({ project, enter, live, reduce }: VisualProps) {
    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
    const touched = useRef(false);
    const x = useTransform(enter, [0, 1], ["12%", "0%"]);
    const r = useTransform(enter, [0, 1], [-3, 0]);
    const src = project.image;
    const on = open && !reduce;
    const word = project.name.toUpperCase();
    const STRIPS = 5;

    if (!src) return <Missing project={project} />;

    return (
        <motion.div style={live ? { x, rotate: r } : undefined} className="relative">
            <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={open}
                aria-label={`${project.name} screenshot. Toggle the split view`}
                onPointerDown={(e) => {
                    touched.current = e.pointerType !== "mouse";
                }}
                onPointerEnter={(e) => e.pointerType === "mouse" && setOpen(true)}
                onPointerLeave={(e) => e.pointerType === "mouse" && setOpen(false)}
                onClick={(e) => {
                    if (touched.current || e.detail === 0) setOpen((o) => !o);
                }}
                className="group block w-full cursor-pointer text-left outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-background"
            >
                <Frame ratio={ratioOf(src)}>
                    <span aria-hidden className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col justify-between py-[2%]">
                        {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
                            <span
                                key={i}
                                style={{
                                    fontSize: "18cqw",
                                    transform: `translateX(${(i % 2 ? -1 : 1) * (on ? 9 : 0)}%)`,
                                    transitionDelay: `${i * 60}ms`,
                                }}
                                className={`ed-outline-text block whitespace-nowrap font-semibold leading-none tracking-tight transition-transform duration-700 ${EASE} motion-reduce:transition-none`}
                            >
                                {`${word} ${word} ${word}`}
                            </span>
                        ))}
                    </span>

                    <span className="absolute inset-0 grid grid-rows-5">
                        {Array.from({ length: STRIPS }, (_, i) => {
                            const dir = i % 2 ? 1 : -1;
                            return (
                                <span
                                    key={i}
                                    style={{
                                        transform: on ? `translateX(${dir * (5 + i * 1.5)}%) scaleY(0.72)` : "none",
                                        transitionDelay: `${i * 45}ms`,
                                    }}
                                    className={`relative block overflow-hidden transition-transform duration-700 ${EASE} motion-reduce:transition-none`}
                                >
                                    <img
                                        src={src}
                                        alt={i === 0 ? (project.imageAlt ?? `${project.name} screenshot`) : ""}
                                        aria-hidden={i === 0 ? undefined : true}
                                        loading="lazy"
                                        decoding="async"
                                        draggable={false}
                                        className="absolute left-0 w-full max-w-none object-cover"
                                        style={{ height: `${STRIPS * 100}%`, top: `-${i * 100}%` }}
                                    />
                                </span>
                            );
                        })}
                    </span>

                    <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-3 left-3 z-10 rounded-full border border-border bg-background px-3 py-1 font-mono text-xs text-foreground/80">
                        {open ? "tap or move away to close" : "hover or tap: open the frames"}
                    </span>
                </Frame>
            </button>
        </motion.div>
    );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Pokeverse: the card tips in from depth as you scroll, then follows the
   cursor in 3D with a soft glare.
   --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
export function PokeverseVisual({ project, enter, live, reduce }: VisualProps) {
    const px = useMotionValue(0.5);
    const py = useMotionValue(0.5);
    const sx = useSpring(px, { stiffness: 170, damping: 20 });
    const sy = useSpring(py, { stiffness: 170, damping: 20 });
    const tiltX = useTransform(sy, [0, 1], [8, -8]);
    const tiltY = useTransform(sx, [0, 1], [-10, 10]);
    const lead = useTransform(enter, [0, 1], [24, 0]);
    const rotX = useTransform([tiltX, lead], (v: number[]) => v[0] + v[1]);
    const y = useTransform(enter, [0, 1], [80, 0]);
    const fx = useTransform(sx, (v) => v * 100);
    const fy = useTransform(sy, (v) => v * 100);
    const glare = useMotionTemplate`radial-gradient(circle at ${fx}% ${fy}%, rgba(255,255,255,0.26), transparent 55%)`;
    const src = project.image;

    if (!src) return <Missing project={project} />;

    return (
        <motion.div
            style={live ? { y } : undefined}
            onPointerMove={(e) => {
                if (e.pointerType !== "mouse" || reduce) return;
                const r = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
                px.set(clamp01((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width));
                py.set(clamp01((e.clientY - r.top) / r.height));
            }}
            onPointerLeave={() => {
                px.set(0.5);
                py.set(0.5);
            }}
            className="[perspective:1100px]"
        >
            <motion.div
                style={{
                    rotateX: reduce ? 0 : live ? rotX : tiltX,
                    rotateY: reduce ? 0 : tiltY,
                    transformStyle: "preserve-3d",
                }}
                className="relative"
            >
                <Frame ratio={ratioOf(src)}>
                    <img
                        src={src}
                        alt={project.imageAlt ?? `${project.name} screenshot`}
                        width={IMAGE_META[src]?.w}
                        height={IMAGE_META[src]?.h}
                        loading="lazy"
                        decoding="async"
                        draggable={false}
                        className="absolute inset-0 size-full object-cover"
                    />
                    <motion.span aria-hidden style={{ background: glare }} className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 mix-blend-soft-light" />
                </Frame>
            </motion.div>
        </motion.div>
    );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Calculate: the screenshot wipes in from the top, then a lens of full colour
   follows the pointer over a muted copy of the same image.
   --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
export function CalculateVisual({ project, enter, live, reduce }: VisualProps) {
    const mx = useMotionValue(0);
    const my = useMotionValue(0);
    const rr = useSpring(0, { stiffness: 200, damping: 24 });
    const rad = useTransform(rr, (v) => Math.max(0, v));
    const mask = useMotionTemplate`radial-gradient(circle ${rad}px at ${mx}px ${my}px, #000 0, #000 72%, transparent 100%)`;
    const wipe = useTransform(enter, [0, 1], ["inset(0% 0% 100% 0%)", "inset(0% 0% 0% 0%)"]);
    const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const src = project.image;

    if (!src) return <Missing project={project} />;

    const track = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
        if (reduce || !ref.current) return;
        const r = ref.current.getBoundingClientRect();
        mx.set(e.clientX - r.left);
        my.set(e.clientY - r.top);
    };

    return (
        <motion.div style={live ? { clipPath: wipe } : undefined}>
            <div
                ref={ref}
                style={{ touchAction: "pan-y" }}
                onPointerEnter={(e) => {
                    track(e);
                    if (!reduce) rr.set(150);
                }}
                onPointerMove={track}
                onPointerLeave={() => rr.set(0)}
                onPointerUp={(e) => {
                    if (e.pointerType !== "mouse") window.setTimeout(() => rr.set(0), 1200);
                }}
            >
                <Frame ratio={ratioOf(src)}>
                    <img
                        src={src}
                        alt={project.imageAlt ?? `${project.name} screenshot`}
                        width={IMAGE_META[src]?.w}
                        height={IMAGE_META[src]?.h}
                        loading="lazy"
                        decoding="async"
                        draggable={false}
                        className="absolute inset-0 size-full object-cover saturate-[0.35] brightness-90"
                    />
                    <motion.img
                        src={src}
                        alt=""
                        aria-hidden
                        loading="lazy"
                        decoding="async"
                        draggable={false}
                        className="absolute inset-0 size-full object-cover"
                        style={{ maskImage: mask, WebkitMaskImage: mask }}
                    />
                    <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-3 left-3 z-10 rounded-full border border-border bg-background px-3 py-1 font-mono text-xs text-foreground/80">
                        move over it to bring it into focus
                    </span>
                </Frame>
            </div>
        </motion.div>
    );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   AlgoForge: the real uploaded screenshots, at their own aspect ratios,
   layered like sheets. They slide in one after another and drift at different
   speeds while the next project covers this one. Hover, focus or click brings
   one to the front. Small screens get a snap-scrolling strip instead.
   --------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
const POS: Record<number, { l: number; t: number; w: number }[]> = {
    1: [{ l: 0, t: 0, w: 100 }],
    2: [
        { l: 0, t: 0, w: 78 },
        { l: 22, t: 34, w: 78 },
    ],
    3: [
        { l: 0, t: 0, w: 70 },
        { l: 30, t: 26, w: 68 },
        { l: 6, t: 54, w: 52 },
    ],
    4: [
        { l: 0, t: 0, w: 70 },
        { l: 30, t: 22, w: 66 },
        { l: 4, t: 50, w: 52 },
        { l: 50, t: 54, w: 46 },
    ],
};

type ShotCardProps = {
    src: string;
    k: number;
    total: number;
    pos: { l: number; t: number; w: number };
    front: number;
    onFront: (k: number) => void;
    enter: MotionValue<number>;
    exit: MotionValue<number>;
    live: boolean;
    name: string;
};

function ShotCard({ src, k, total, pos, front, onFront, enter, exit, live, name }: ShotCardProps) {
    const a = Math.min(0.5, k * 0.12);
    const x = useTransform(enter, [a, a + 0.5], ["26%", "0%"]);
    const o = useTransform(enter, [a, a + 0.4], [0, 1]);
    const lift = useTransform(exit, [0, 1], [0, -(k + 1) * 16]);
    const motionStyle = live ? { x, opacity: o, y: lift } : {};

    return (
        <motion.div
            style={{ left: `${pos.l}%`, top: `${pos.t}%`, width: `${pos.w}%`, zIndex: front === k ? 20 : 10 - k, ...motionStyle }}
            className="absolute"
        >
            <button
                type="button"
                aria-label={`Bring ${name} screenshot ${k + 1} of ${total} to the front`}
                aria-pressed={front === k}
                onClick={() => onFront(k)}
                onFocus={() => onFront(k)}
                onPointerEnter={(e) => e.pointerType === "mouse" && onFront(k)}
                className={`block w-full cursor-pointer outline-none transition-transform duration-500 ${EASE} hover:scale-[1.02] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-background motion-reduce:transition-none`}
            >
                <span className={`block overflow-hidden rounded-[6px] border border-border bg-background ${SHADOW}`}>
                    <Shot src={src} alt={`${name} screenshot ${k + 1}`} eager={k === 0} />
                </span>
            </button>
        </motion.div>
    );
}

export function AlgoForgeVisual({ project, enter, exit, live }: VisualProps) {
    const all = project.shots ?? [];
    const [front, setFront] = useState(0);
    const shots = all.slice(0, 4);
    const layout = POS[Math.min(4, shots.length)];

    if (shots.length === 0) return <Missing project={project} />;

    return (
        <div>
            <div className="relative hidden aspect-[5/4] w-full lg:block">
                {shots.map((src, k) => (
                    <ShotCard
                        key={src}
                        src={src}
                        k={k}
                        total={all.length}
                        pos={layout[k]}
                        front={front}
                        onFront={setFront}
                        enter={enter}
                        exit={exit}
                        live={live}
                        name={project.name}
                    />
                ))}
            </div>

            <ul
                aria-label={`${project.name} screenshots`}
                className="-mx-1 flex snap-x snap-mandatory gap-3 overflow-x-auto px-1 pb-2 [scrollbar-width:none] lg:hidden [&::-webkit-scrollbar]:hidden"
            >
                {all.map((src, k) => (
                    <li key={src} className="w-[84%] shrink-0 snap-center sm:w-[70%]">
                        <span className={`block overflow-hidden rounded-[6px] border border-border bg-background ${SHADOW}`}>
                            <Shot src={src} alt={`${project.name} screenshot ${k + 1}`} eager={k === 0} />
                        </span>
                    </li>
                ))}
            </ul>

            <p className="mt-3 font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground" aria-live="polite">
                <span className="hidden lg:inline">
                    Screenshot {front + 1} of {all.length}
                    {all.length > shots.length ? ` (${shots.length} shown)` : ""}
                </span>
                <span className="lg:hidden">{all.length} screenshots, swipe sideways</span>
            </p>
        </div>
    );
}

function GenericVisual({ project }: VisualProps) {
    const src = project.image;
    if (!src) return <Missing project={project} />;
    return (
        <Frame ratio={ratioOf(src)}>
            <img src={src} alt={project.imageAlt ?? `${project.name} screenshot`} loading="lazy" decoding="async" className="absolute inset-0 size-full object-cover" />
        </Frame>
    );
}

export const VISUALS: Record<string, ComponentType<VisualProps>> = {
    hermes: HermesVisual,
    pokeverse: PokeverseVisual,
    calculate: CalculateVisual,
    algoforge: AlgoForgeVisual,
};

export const visualFor = (id: string): ComponentType<VisualProps> => VISUALS[id] ?? GenericVisual;

FILEPATH: ./src/components/section/skill/Skills.tsx

"use client";
/* redesign:v1 */

import {
    useCallback,
    useEffect,
    useMemo,
    useRef,
    useState,
    type CSSProperties,
    type KeyboardEvent,
} from "react";
import {
    AnimatePresence,
    LayoutGroup,
    animate,
    motion,
    useInView,
    useMotionValue,
    useReducedMotion,
} from "framer-motion";
import { HugeiconsIcon } from "@hugeicons/react";
import { ArrowRight01Icon, RefreshIcon } from "@hugeicons/core-free-icons";
import Shapes from "@/components/ui/Shapes"
import SkillTile from "@/components/section/skill/SkillTile";
import {
    copy,
    CATEGORIES,
    LEVELS,
    SKILLS,
    SKILL_BY_ID,
    CAT_BY_ID,
    COLS,
    type FilterId,
    type Skill,

import ConfettiLayer from "@/components/ui/ConfettiLayer";

const tintOf = (skill: Skill) => CAT_BY_ID[skill.cat].tint;

/* ==========================================================================

   One entrance: the heading wipes in, then the tiles pop onto the board.
   Everything after that is a response to something the visitor does.

   and as a small pop when you open a "Daily driver" skill.
