/* Segmented control with a pill that glides between options. */
function FilterBar({
    value,
    onChange,
    counts,
    reduce,
}: {
    value: FilterId;
    onChange: (id: FilterId) => void;
    counts: Record<string, number>;
    reduce: boolean;
}) {
    return (
        <div
            role="group"
            aria-label="Filter skills by category"
            className="flex max-w-full items-center gap-1 overflow-x-auto rounded-full border border-border bg-muted/50 p-1 [scrollbar-width:none] [&::-webkit-scrollbar]:hidden"
        >
            {CATEGORIES.map((c) => {
                const active = c.id === value;
                return (
                    <button
                        key={c.id}
                        type="button"
                        aria-pressed={active}
                        onClick={() => onChange(c.id)}
                        className="relative inline-flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-3 text-[13px] font-medium outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-background sm:h-9 sm:px-3.5 sm:text-sm"
                    >
                        {active && (
                            <motion.span
                                layoutId="filter-pill"
                                transition={reduce ? { duration: 0 } : softSpring}
                                className="absolute inset-0 rounded-full bg-foreground"
                            />
                        )}
                        <span
                            aria-hidden
                            className="relative size-1.5 rounded-full"
                            style={{ background: c.tint }}
                        />
                        <span
                            className={`relative transition-colors duration-200 ${
                                active ? "text-background" : "text-foreground/80"
                            }`}
                        >
                            {c.label}
                        </span>
                        <span
                            className={`relative text-xs tabular-nums pt-1 transition-colors duration-200 ${
                                active ? "text-background/70" : "text-muted-foreground"
                            }`}
                        >
                            {counts[c.id]}
                        </span>
                    </button>
                );
            })}
        </div>
    );
}

/* ==========================================================================
   LEVEL METER
   ========================================================================== */

function LevelMeter({
    level,
    tint,
    reduce,
}: {
    level: number;
    tint: string;
    reduce: boolean;
}) {
    return (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div
                role="img"
                aria-label={`${LEVELS[level - 1]}, level ${level} of 4`}
                className="flex flex-1 gap-1.5"
            >
                {Array.from({ length: 4 }, (_, i) => (
                    <span
                        key={i}
                        className="relative h-2 flex-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--foreground)_10%,transparent)]"
                    >
                        {i < level && (
                            <motion.span
                                initial={reduce ? false : { scaleX: 0 }}
                                animate={{ scaleX: 1 }}
                                transition={{ ...spring, delay: 0.15 + i * 0.09 }}
                                className="absolute inset-0 origin-left rounded-full"
                                style={{ background: tint }}
                            />
                        )}
                    </span>
                ))}
            </div>
            <span className="w-24 shrink-0 text-right text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                {LEVELS[level - 1]}
            </span>
        </div>
    );
}

/* ==========================================================================
   SKILL TILE
   Implemented in SkillTile.tsx.
   ========================================================================== */

/* ==========================================================================
   INSPECTOR   INSPECTOR
   Shows how I use the selected tool and what it pairs with.
   ========================================================================== */

function Inspector({
    skill,
    reduce,
    onPick,
}: {
    skill: Skill | null;
    reduce: boolean;
    onPick: (id: string) => void;
}) {
    const tint = skill ? tintOf(skill) : "var(--primary)";
    const tintVars = { "--tint": tint } as CSSProperties;

    return (
        <div
            aria-live="polite"
            style={tintVars}
            className="relative h-48 min-h-0 lg:h-auto lg:flex-1"
        >
            <AnimatePresence mode="wait" initial={false}>
                {skill ? (
                    <motion.article
                        key={skill.id}
                        initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 14, rotate: -1.5 }}
                        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, rotate: 0 }}
                        exit={reduce ? undefined : { opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
                        transition={spring}
                        className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_40%,transparent)] bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_12%,var(--background))] p-3.5 sm:p-5"
                    >
                        <span
                            aria-hidden
                            className="absolute -top-0.5 left-1/2 hidden h-5 w-14 -translate-x-1/2 -rotate-2 rounded-b-[3px] bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_38%,transparent)] lg:block"
                        />

                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                            <motion.span
                                initial={reduce ? false : { rotate: -18, scale: 0.6 }}
                                animate={{ rotate: 0, scale: 1 }}
                                transition={bouncy}
                                className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_24%,transparent)] text-sm font-semibold text-[color:var(--tint)] sm:size-12 sm:text-base"
                            >
                                {skill.mark}
                            </motion.span>

                            <div className="min-w-0">
                                <h3 className="truncate text-base font-semibold leading-tight tracking-tight sm:text-xl">
                                    {skill.name}
                                </h3>
                                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                                    {CAT_BY_ID[skill.cat].label}
                                </p>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <p className="mt-2.5 line-clamp-2 text-[13px] leading-5 text-foreground/80 sm:mt-3 sm:line-clamp-3 sm:text-sm sm:leading-6 lg:mt-4 lg:line-clamp-4">
                            {skill.note}
                        </p>

                        <div className="mt-auto flex flex-col gap-3 pt-2">
                            <LevelMeter level={skill.level} tint={tint} reduce={reduce} />

                            {skill.usedIn && (
                                <p className="hidden items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground lg:flex">
                                    <span className="font-medium text-foreground/80">Used in</span>
                                    {skill.usedIn}
                                </p>
                            )}

                            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto [scrollbar-width:none] lg:flex-wrap lg:overflow-visible">
                                <span className="shrink-0 text-xs font-medium text-foreground/80">
                                    Pairs with
                                </span>
                                {skill.related.map((rid) => {
                                    const r = SKILL_BY_ID[rid];
                                    if (!r) return null;
                                    return (
                                        <motion.button
                                            key={rid}
                                            type="button"
                                            onClick={() => onPick(rid)}
                                            whileHover={reduce ? undefined : { y: -2 }}
                                            whileTap={reduce ? undefined : { scale: 0.94 }}
                                            transition={spring}
                                            style={{ "--chip": tintOf(r) } as CSSProperties}
                                            className="inline-flex h-7 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--chip)_40%,transparent)] bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--chip)_10%,transparent)] px-2.5 text-xs font-medium outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary"
                                        >
                                            {r.name}
                                            <HugeiconsIcon icon={ArrowRight01Icon} size={12} strokeWidth={2} />
                                        </motion.button>
                                    );
                                })}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </motion.article>
                ) : (
                    <motion.div
                        key="empty"
                        initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0 }}
                        animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                        exit={reduce ? undefined : { opacity: 0 }}
                        className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-border bg-muted/30 p-4 text-center"
                    >
                        <motion.span
                            aria-hidden
                            animate={reduce ? undefined : { x: [0, -6, 0] }}
                            transition={{ duration: 1.6, repeat: Infinity, ease: "easeInOut" }}
                            className="flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-primary"
                        >
                            <HugeiconsIcon
                                icon={ArrowRight01Icon}
                                size={18}
                                strokeWidth={2}
                                className="-rotate-90 lg:rotate-180"
                            />
                        </motion.span>
                        <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{copy.emptyTitle}</p>
                        <p className="max-w-[14rem] text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
                            {copy.emptyText}
                        </p>
                    </motion.div>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
    );
}

/* ==========================================================================
   EXPLORE BAR
   A progress ring that fills as you look at each tool, a rank that grows,
   and a "Surprise me" button that scans the board and lands on one.
   ========================================================================== */

function ProgressRing({ value, reduce }: { value: number; reduce: boolean }) {
    return (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 40 40" aria-hidden className="size-10 shrink-0 -rotate-90">
            <circle
                cx="20"
                cy="20"
                r="16"
                fill="none"
                stroke="currentColor"
                strokeWidth="4"
                className="text-border"
            />
            <motion.circle
                cx="20"
                cy="20"
                r="16"
                fill="none"
                stroke="var(--primary)"
                strokeWidth="4"
                strokeLinecap="round"
                initial={false}
                animate={{ pathLength: value }}
                transition={reduce ? { duration: 0 } : softSpring}
            />
        </svg>
    );
}

function ExploreBar({
    explored,
    total,
    busy,
    onSurprise,
    reduce,
    hint,
}: {
    explored: number;
    total: number;
    busy: boolean;
    onSurprise: () => void;
    reduce: boolean;
    hint: string;
}) {
    const done = explored === total;
    const rank = done
        ? "Completionist"
        : explored >= total / 2
          ? "Explorer"
          : explored > 0
            ? "Curious"
            : "Fresh eyes";

    return (
        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3">
            <ProgressRing value={explored / total} reduce={reduce} />

            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <p className="truncate text-sm font-medium" aria-live="polite">
                    {done ? copy.done : `${explored} of ${total} · ${rank}`}
                </p>
                <p className="hidden truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground sm:block">{hint}</p>
            </div>

            <motion.button
                type="button"
                onClick={onSurprise}
                disabled={busy}
                whileHover={reduce || busy ? undefined : { y: -2 }}
                whileTap={reduce || busy ? undefined : { scale: 0.94 }}
                transition={spring}
                className="inline-flex h-9 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-border bg-background px-3.5 text-sm font-medium outline-none hover:bg-muted/60 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary disabled:opacity-60 sm:h-10 sm:px-4"
            >
                <motion.span
                    aria-hidden
                    animate={busy && !reduce ? { rotate: 360 } : { rotate: 0 }}
                    transition={
                        busy && !reduce
                            ? { duration: 0.7, repeat: Infinity, ease: "linear" }
                            : softSpring
                    }
                    className="flex"
                >
                    <HugeiconsIcon icon={RefreshIcon} size={16} strokeWidth={2} />
                </motion.span>
                Surprise me
            </motion.button>
        </div>
    );
}

/* ==========================================================================
   SECTION
   Phones and tablets: the section grows with its content, tiles stay square.
   Desktop (lg+): exactly one screen, never taller than the viewport.
   ========================================================================== */

export default function Skills() {
    const reduce = !!useReducedMotion();
    const fine = useFinePointer();
    const initial = reduce ? "show" : "hidden";

    const sectionRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
    const gridRef = useRef<HTMLUListElement>(null);
    const ready = useInView(sectionRef, { once: true, amount: 0.3 });
    const { bursts, fire } = useConfetti(!reduce);

    const [filter, setFilter] = useState<FilterId>("all");
    const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [explored, setExplored] = useState<string[]>([]);
    const [scanId, setScanId] = useState<string | null>(null);


    const visible = useMemo(
        () => (filter === "all" ? SKILLS : SKILLS.filter((s) => s.cat === filter)),
        [filter],
    );

    const counts = useMemo(() => {
        const c: Record<string, number> = { all: SKILLS.length };
        for (const s of SKILLS) c[s.cat] = (c[s.cat] ?? 0) + 1;
        return c;
    }, []);

    const selected = selectedId ? SKILL_BY_ID[selectedId] : null;
    const relatedSet = useMemo(
        () => new Set(selected ? [selected.id, ...selected.related] : []),
        [selected],
    );

    const pick = useCallback(
        (id: string) => {
            const s = SKILL_BY_ID[id];
            setSelectedId(id);
            setExplored((e) => (e.includes(id) ? e : [...e, id]));
            // if the pick lives in a different category, switch so you can see it
            setFilter((f) => (f === "all" || f === s.cat ? f : s.cat));

            // daily drivers earn a tiny pop
            if (s.level === 4) {
                const el = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-skill="${id}"]`);
                const r = el?.getBoundingClientRect();
                if (r) fire(r.left + r.width / 2, r.top + r.height / 2, 12);
            }
        },
        [fire],
    );

    // "Surprise me": scan the visible tiles, slow down, land on one
    const timers = useRef<number[]>([]);
    const busy = scanId !== null;

    useEffect(
        () => () => {
            timers.current.forEach((t) => window.clearTimeout(t));
        },
        [],
    );

    function surprise() {
        if (busy || visible.length === 0) return;

        const unexplored = visible.filter(
            (s) => !explored.includes(s.id) && s.id !== selectedId,
        );
        const pool = unexplored.length ? unexplored : visible;
        const target = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)];

        if (reduce) {
            pick(target.id);
            return;
        }

        const start = Math.floor(Math.random() * visible.length);
        const steps = 9;
        let t = 0;

        for (let k = 0; k < steps; k++) {
            const id = visible[(start + k) % visible.length].id;
            t += 55 + k * 22;
            timers.current.push(window.setTimeout(() => setScanId(id), t));
        }

        t += 260;
        timers.current.push(window.setTimeout(() => setScanId(target.id), t));
        t += 360;
        timers.current.push(
            window.setTimeout(() => {
                setScanId(null);
                pick(target.id);
            }, t),
        );
    }

    // easter egg: explore everything and the board celebrates
    const allDone = explored.length === SKILLS.length;
    const wasDone = useRef(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (allDone && !wasDone.current) {
            const r = gridRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
            if (r) fire(r.left + r.width / 2, r.top + r.height / 2, 36);
        }
        wasDone.current = allDone;
    }, [allDone, fire]);

    // roving keyboard navigation across the tile grid
    function onGridKey(e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLUListElement>) {
        if (e.key === "Escape") {
            setSelectedId(null);
            return;
        }

        const items = Array.from(
            e.currentTarget.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>("[data-tile]"),
        );
        const idx = items.indexOf(document.activeElement as HTMLButtonElement);
        if (idx < 0) return;

        let next = idx;
        if (e.key === "ArrowRight") next = idx + 1;
        else if (e.key === "ArrowLeft") next = idx - 1;
        else if (e.key === "ArrowDown") next = idx + COLS;
        else if (e.key === "ArrowUp") next = idx - COLS;
        else if (e.key === "Home") next = 0;
        else if (e.key === "End") next = items.length - 1;
        else return;

        e.preventDefault();
        items[clamp(next, 0, items.length - 1)]?.focus();
    }

    // nudge the hint so it matches how you interact
    const hint = fine ? copy.hintFine : copy.hintTouch;

    // fade the board's dashed frame in once the section is on screen
    const glowOpacity = useMotionValue(0);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!ready || reduce) return;
        const controls = animate(glowOpacity, 1, { duration: 1.2, delay: 0.3 });
        return () => controls.stop();
    }, [ready, reduce, glowOpacity]);

    return (
        <section
            id="skills"
            ref={sectionRef}
            
            className="relative box-border flex min-h-svh w-full  flex-col overflow-hidden border-t border-border bg-background px-4 pb-6 pt-12 sm:px-6 lg:h-svh lg:pb-8 lg:pt-16"
        >
            <Shapes reduce={reduce} />

            <div className="relative mx-auto flex w-full max-w-6xl flex-1 flex-col gap-5 lg:min-h-0 lg:gap-7">
                {/* ---------- header ---------- */}
                <SectionHeader
                    index="02"
                    label={copy.badge}
                    top={copy.headingTop}
                    bottom={copy.headingBottom}
                    reduce={reduce}
                    aside={
                        <FilterBar value={filter} onChange={setFilter} counts={counts} reduce={reduce} />
                    }
                />

                {/* ---------- board + inspector ---------- */}
                <div className="grid flex-1 gap-4 lg:min-h-0 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_19rem] lg:gap-6">
                    <LayoutGroup>
                        <div className="relative flex flex-col lg:min-h-0">
                            <motion.div
                                style={{ opacity: reduce ? 1 : glowOpacity }}
                                className="relative rounded-[1.5rem] border border-dashed border-border p-3 sm:rounded-[2rem] sm:p-5 lg:min-h-0 lg:flex-1"
                            >
                                <ul
                                    ref={gridRef}
                                    onKeyDown={onGridKey}
                                    className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 sm:gap-3 lg:h-full lg:grid-rows-4 lg:gap-4"
                                >
                                    <AnimatePresence mode="popLayout" initial={false}>
                                        {visible.map((skill, i) => (
                                            <SkillTile
                                                key={skill.id}
                                                skill={skill}
                                                index={i}
                                                selected={selectedId === skill.id}
                                                dimmed={!!selected && !relatedSet.has(skill.id)}
                                                related={
                                                    !!selected && selected.related.includes(skill.id)
                                                }
                                                scanning={scanId === skill.id}
                                                explored={explored.includes(skill.id)}
                                                ready={ready}
                                                reduce={reduce}
                                                fine={fine}
                                                onSelect={pick}
                                            />
                                        ))}
                                    </AnimatePresence>
                                </ul>
                            </motion.div>

                            <p className="mt-2 hidden truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground sm:block">
                                {CAT_BY_ID[filter].blurb}
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </LayoutGroup>

                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 lg:min-h-0 lg:gap-4">
                        <Inspector skill={selected} reduce={reduce} onPick={pick} />
                        <ExploreBar
                            explored={explored.length}
                            total={SKILLS.length}
                            busy={busy}
                            onSurprise={surprise}
                            reduce={reduce}
                            hint={hint}
                        />
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <ConfettiLayer bursts={bursts} />
        </section>
    );
}

========================================================================
FILEPATH: ./src/components/section/skill/SkillTile.tsx
========================================================================
"use client";

import { useRef, useState, type CSSProperties, type PointerEvent as ReactPointerEvent } from "react";
import { motion, useMotionTemplate, useMotionValue, useSpring, useTransform, type Variants } from "framer-motion";
import { CAT_BY_ID, LEVELS, type Skill } from "@/content/skills";
import { bouncy, softSpring } from "@/lib/motion";

const tiltSpring = { stiffness: 220, damping: 16 } as const;

type TileCustom = { i: number; tilt: number };

const tileVariants: Variants = {
    hidden: { opacity: 0, scale: 0.55, y: 26, rotate: -8 },
    show: ({ i, tilt }: TileCustom) => ({
        opacity: 1,
        scale: 1,
        y: 0,
        rotate: tilt,
        transition: { type: "spring", stiffness: 260, damping: 17, delay: 0.25 + i * 0.045 },
    }),
    exit: { opacity: 0, scale: 0.6, transition: { duration: 0.18 } },
};

const WAVE = "color-mix(in oklab, var(--tint) 40%, transparent)";

/** Four pips = four levels. Filled pips are the skill level. */
function Pips({ level }: { level: number }) {
    return (
        <span aria-hidden className="flex gap-[3px]">
            {Array.from({ length: 4 }, (_, i) => (
                <span
                    key={i}
                    className={`size-1 rounded-full sm:size-1.5 ${
                        i < level ? "bg-[color:var(--tint)]" : "bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--foreground)_18%,transparent)]"
                    }`}
                />
            ))}
        </span>
    );
}

/** A liquid that rises to the skill level on hover or selection. */
function Liquid({ level, open }: { level: number; open: boolean }) {
    return (
        <span
            aria-hidden
            style={{ "--fill": `${level * 24}%` } as CSSProperties}
            className={`pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-b-2xl transition-[height] duration-700 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.2,0.8,0.2,1)] motion-reduce:transition-none ${
                open
                    ? "h-[var(--fill)]"
                    : "h-[8%] group-hover:h-[var(--fill)] group-focus-visible:h-[var(--fill)]"
            }`}
        >
            <svg
                viewBox="0 0 200 8"
                preserveAspectRatio="none"
                className="rd-wave -mb-px block h-2 w-[200%] shrink-0"
                style={{ color: WAVE }}
                fill="currentColor"
            >
                <path d="M0 4 Q12.5 0 25 4 T50 4 T75 4 T100 4 T125 4 T150 4 T175 4 T200 4 V8 H0Z" />
            </svg>
            <span className="block flex-1" style={{ background: WAVE }} />
        </span>
    );
}

export default function SkillTile({
    skill,
    index,
    selected,
    dimmed,
    related,
    scanning,
    explored,
    ready,
    reduce,
    fine,
    onSelect,
}: {
    skill: Skill;
    index: number;
    selected: boolean;
    dimmed: boolean;
    related: boolean;
    scanning: boolean;
    explored: boolean;
    ready: boolean;
    reduce: boolean;
    fine: boolean;
    onSelect: (id: string) => void;
}) {
    const tint = CAT_BY_ID[skill.cat].tint;
    const tilt = [-1.6, 1.2, -0.8, 1.6][index % 4];
    const canDrag = fine && !reduce;

    const [dragging, setDragging] = useState(false);
    const moved = useRef(false);

    const gx = useMotionValue(50);
    const gy = useMotionValue(50);
    const px = useMotionValue(0);
    const py = useMotionValue(0);
    const rotX = useSpring(useTransform(py, [-0.5, 0.5], [9, -9]), tiltSpring);
    const rotY = useSpring(useTransform(px, [-0.5, 0.5], [-9, 9]), tiltSpring);
    const glow = useMotionTemplate`radial-gradient(110px circle at ${gx}px ${gy}px, color-mix(in oklab, var(--tint) 34%, transparent), transparent 72%)`;

    function onMove(e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) {
        if (!fine || reduce) return;
        const r = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
        gx.set(e.clientX - r.left);
        gy.set(e.clientY - r.top);
        px.set((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width - 0.5);
        py.set((e.clientY - r.top) / r.height - 0.5);
    }

    function onLeave() {
        px.set(0);
        py.set(0);
    }

    const vars = { "--tint": tint, zIndex: dragging ? 30 : selected ? 5 : 1 } as CSSProperties;

    return (
        <motion.li
            layout={!reduce}
            custom={{ i: index, tilt } satisfies TileCustom}
            variants={tileVariants}
            initial={reduce ? false : "hidden"}
            animate={ready || reduce ? "show" : "hidden"}
            exit="exit"
            transition={reduce ? { duration: 0 } : { layout: softSpring }}
            style={vars}
            className="relative aspect-square min-h-0 lg:aspect-auto"
        >
            <motion.button
                type="button"
                data-tile
                data-skill={skill.id}
                aria-pressed={selected}
                aria-label={`${skill.name}, ${LEVELS[skill.level - 1]}`}
                onClick={() => {
                    if (moved.current) {
                        moved.current = false;
                        return;
                    }
                    onSelect(skill.id);
                }}
                onPointerMove={onMove}
                onPointerLeave={onLeave}
                drag={canDrag}
                dragSnapToOrigin
                dragElastic={0.4}
                dragMomentum={false}
                onDragStart={() => {
                    moved.current = true;
                    setDragging(true);
                }}
                onDragEnd={() => setDragging(false)}
                whileHover={reduce ? undefined : { y: -4, scale: 1.04 }}
                whileTap={reduce ? undefined : { scale: 0.95 }}
                whileDrag={{ scale: 1.1, rotate: 4 }}
                style={canDrag ? { rotateX: rotX, rotateY: rotY, transformPerspective: 600 } : undefined}
                transition={bouncy}
                className="group relative block h-full w-full touch-manipulation rounded-2xl outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[color:var(--tint)] focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-background"
            >
                <span
                    className={`absolute inset-0 flex flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-2 bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_8%,var(--background))] p-2 text-left shadow-[2px_2px_0_0_color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_55%,transparent)] transition-[opacity,filter,border-color,box-shadow] duration-300 group-hover:shadow-[4px_4px_0_0_var(--tint)] sm:p-3 ${
                        selected
                            ? "border-[color:var(--tint)] shadow-[4px_4px_0_0_var(--tint)]"
                            : "border-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_34%,transparent)] group-hover:border-[color:var(--tint)]"
                    } ${dimmed ? "opacity-35 saturate-50" : ""}`}
                >
                    <Liquid level={skill.level} open={selected} />

                    <motion.span
                        aria-hidden
                        style={{ background: glow }}
                        className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 opacity-0 transition-opacity duration-200 group-hover:opacity-100"
                    />

                    <span className="relative flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <span className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--tint)_24%,transparent)] text-xs font-bold tracking-tight text-[color:var(--tint)] transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:-rotate-6 group-hover:scale-110 sm:size-10 sm:rounded-xl sm:text-sm">
                            {skill.mark}
                        </span>
                        {skill.level === 4 && (
                            <span aria-hidden className="rd-twinkle text-xs text-[color:var(--tint)] sm:text-xs">
                                ✦
                            </span>
                        )}
                    </span>

                    <span className="relative flex flex-col gap-1">
                        <span className="block truncate text-xs font-semibold leading-tight sm:text-sm">
                            {skill.name}
                        </span>
                        <Pips level={skill.level} />
                    </span>

                    <motion.span
                        aria-hidden
                        initial={false}
                        animate={{ scale: explored ? 1 : 0, opacity: explored ? 1 : 0 }}
                        transition={reduce ? { duration: 0 } : bouncy}
                        className="absolute right-1.5 top-1.5 flex size-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-(--tint) sm:right-2 sm:top-2 sm:size-5"
                    >
                        <svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" className="size-2.5 text-background">
                            <path d="M3.5 8.5l3 3 6-7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.4" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" />
                        </svg>
                    </motion.span>

                    {related && !selected && (
                        <span aria-hidden className="absolute bottom-2 right-2 flex size-2">
                            <span className="absolute inline-flex size-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-[color:var(--tint)] opacity-60 motion-reduce:animate-none" />
                            <span className="relative inline-flex size-2 rounded-full bg-[color:var(--tint)]" />
                        </span>
                    )}
                </span>

                {selected && (
                    <motion.span
                        aria-hidden
                        layoutId="skill-ring"
                        transition={reduce ? { duration: 0 } : softSpring}
                        className="pointer-events-none absolute -inset-1.5 rounded-[1.25rem] border-2 border-dashed border-[color:var(--tint)]"
                    />
                )}

                {scanning && (
                    <span aria-hidden className="pointer-events-none absolute -inset-1.5 rounded-[1.25rem] border-2 border-dashed border-[color:var(--tint)]" />
                )}
            </motion.button>
        </motion.li>
    );
}


========================================================================
FILEPATH: ./src/components/ThemeToggle.tsx
========================================================================
"use client";

import type { MouseEvent } from "react";
import { Moon, Sun } from "lucide-react";
import { useTheme } from "next-themes";
import { useMounted } from "@/hooks/useMounted";
import { runViewTransition } from "@/lib/view-transition";

export function ThemeToggle() {
    const { resolvedTheme, setTheme } = useTheme();
    const mounted = useMounted();

    // Reserve the space so the navbar does not jump after mount.
    if (!mounted) return <span aria-hidden className="size-11" />;

    const dark = resolvedTheme !== "light";

    const toggle = (e: MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
        const r = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
        runViewTransition(() => setTheme(dark ? "light" : "dark"), {
            x: r.left + r.width / 2,
            y: r.top + r.height / 2,
        });
    };

    return (
        <button
            type="button"
            onClick={toggle}
            aria-label={dark ? "Switch to light theme" : "Switch to dark theme"}
            className="grid size-11 place-items-center rounded-full outline-none transition-colors hover:bg-muted focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary/60 active:scale-95"
        >
            {dark ? <Sun size={20} /> : <Moon size={20} />}
        </button>
    );
}

========================================================================
FILEPATH: ./src/components/ui/Button.tsx
========================================================================
"use client";
// redesign.sh:v1

import Link from "next/link";
import type { MouseEvent, ReactNode } from "react";
import { HugeiconsIcon } from "@hugeicons/react";
import { ArrowRightIcon } from "@hugeicons/core-free-icons";

type Variant = "solid" | "outline" | "ghost";
type Size = "md" | "sm";

export type ButtonProps = {
    children?: ReactNode;
    variant?: Variant;
    size?: Size;
    href?: string;
    external?: boolean;
    type?: "button" | "submit";
    onClick?: (e: MouseEvent<HTMLElement>) => void;
    disabled?: boolean;
    loading?: boolean;
    iconOnly?: boolean;
    icon?: ReactNode;
    ariaLabel?: string;
    className?: string;
};

/*
 * The site button: a label, then a round arrow chip. On hover the chip's fill
 * grows behind the label and the label flips to the on-fill colour.
 * It reads --tint / --on-tint when a parent sets them (project colours).
 * Hover, focus-visible, active, disabled and loading states are shared by every
 * usage on the site (PlayButton is a thin adapter over this component).
 */
const VARIANTS: Record<Variant, string> = {
    solid:
        "[--btn-fill:var(--tint,var(--primary))] [--btn-on:var(--on-tint,var(--primary-foreground))] [--btn-line:color-mix(in_oklab,var(--foreground)_18%,transparent)]",
    outline:
        "[--btn-fill:var(--foreground)] [--btn-on:var(--background)] [--btn-line:color-mix(in_oklab,var(--foreground)_24%,transparent)]",
    ghost:
        "[--btn-fill:color-mix(in_oklab,var(--foreground)_12%,transparent)] [--btn-on:var(--foreground)] [--btn-line:transparent]",
};

const ROOT =
    "group/btn relative isolate inline-flex shrink-0 select-none items-center gap-3 rounded-full text-sm font-medium text-foreground outline-none " +
    "transition-transform duration-300 active:scale-[0.98] motion-reduce:transition-none " +
    "focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-background " +
    "disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-60 aria-busy:cursor-progress";

const SIZE = {
    md: { box: "h-11 pl-5 pr-1", chip: "size-9", fill: "w-9", icon: "size-11" },
    sm: { box: "h-9 pl-4 pr-1", chip: "size-7", fill: "w-7", icon: "size-9" },
} as const;

function Spinner() {
    return (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden className="size-4 animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none" fill="none">
            <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="3" opacity="0.25" />
            <path d="M21 12a9 9 0 0 0-9-9" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="3" strokeLinecap="round" />
        </svg>
    );
}

export default function Button({
    children,
    variant = "outline",
    size = "md",
    href,
    external,
    type = "button",
    onClick,
    disabled,
    loading,
    iconOnly,
    icon,
    ariaLabel,
    className = "",
}: ButtonProps) {
    const label = children ?? "Read case study";
    const s = SIZE[size];
    const cls = `${ROOT} ${VARIANTS[variant]} ${iconOnly ? `${s.icon} justify-center` : s.box} ${className}`;
    const solid = variant === "solid";

    const inner = (
        <>
            {variant !== "ghost" && (
                <span aria-hidden className="absolute inset-0 -z-10 rounded-full border border-[color:var(--btn-line)]" />
            )}
            <span
                aria-hidden
                className={
                    iconOnly
                        ? `absolute inset-0 -z-10 rounded-full bg-[color:var(--btn-fill)] transition-transform duration-500 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)] motion-reduce:transition-none ${
                              solid
                                  ? "scale-100 group-hover/btn:scale-110"
                                  : "scale-0 group-hover/btn:scale-100 group-focus-visible/btn:scale-100"
                          }`
                        : `absolute inset-y-1 right-1 -z-10 ${s.fill} rounded-full bg-[color:var(--btn-fill)] transition-[width] duration-500 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)] group-hover/btn:w-[calc(100%-0.5rem)] group-focus-visible/btn:w-[calc(100%-0.5rem)] motion-reduce:transition-none`
                }
            />
            {iconOnly ? (
                <span
                    className={`relative z-10 grid place-items-center transition-colors duration-300 ${
                        solid
                            ? "text-[color:var(--btn-on)]"
                            : "text-foreground group-hover/btn:text-[color:var(--btn-on)] group-focus-visible/btn:text-[color:var(--btn-on)]"
                    }`}
                >
                    {loading ? <Spinner /> : label}
                </span>
            ) : (
                <>
                    <span className="relative z-10 inline-flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap transition-colors duration-300 group-hover/btn:text-[color:var(--btn-on)] group-focus-visible/btn:text-[color:var(--btn-on)]">
                        {label}
                    </span>
                    <span className={`relative z-10 grid ${s.chip} shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full text-[color:var(--btn-on)]`}>
                        {loading ? (
                            <Spinner />
                        ) : (
                            (icon ?? (
                                <HugeiconsIcon
                                    icon={ArrowRightIcon}
                                    size={16}
                                    strokeWidth={2}
                                    className="transition-transform duration-300 group-hover/btn:translate-x-0.5 motion-reduce:transition-none"
                                />
                            ))
                        )}
                    </span>
                </>
            )}
        </>
    );

    if (href) {
        const newTab = external === true || (external === undefined && /^https?:/.test(href));
        const plain = newTab || /^(mailto:|tel:)/.test(href);
        if (plain) {
            return (
                <a
                    href={href}
                    target={newTab ? "_blank" : undefined}
                    rel={newTab ? "noopener noreferrer" : undefined}
                    aria-label={ariaLabel}
                    onClick={onClick}
                    className={cls}
                >
                    {inner}
                </a>
            );
        }
        return (
            <Link href={href} aria-label={ariaLabel} onClick={onClick} className={cls}>
                {inner}
            </Link>
        );
    }

    return (
        <button
            type={type}
            disabled={disabled || loading}
            aria-busy={loading || undefined}
            aria-label={ariaLabel}
            onClick={onClick}
            className={cls}
        >
            {inner}
        </button>
    );
}

"use client";

import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import type { Burst } from "@/hooks/useConfetti";

export default function ConfettiLayer({ bursts }: { bursts: Burst[] }) {
    return (
        <div
            aria-hidden
            className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-0 z-[60] overflow-hidden"
        >
            {bursts.map((burst) => (
                <div
                    key={burst.id}
                    className="absolute"
                    style={{ left: burst.x, top: burst.y }}
                >
                    {burst.particles.map((p) => (
                        <motion.span
                            key={p.id}
                            initial={{ x: 0, y: 0, opacity: 1, scale: 0.4, rotate: 0 }}
                            animate={{
                                x: p.dx,
                                y: [0, p.dy, p.dy + 90],
                                opacity: [1, 1, 0],
                                scale: [0.4, 1, 0.8],
                                rotate: p.rot,
                            }}
                            transition={{ duration: 1.1, ease: "easeOut", times: [0, 0.55, 1] }}
                            className={`absolute block ${p.round ? "rounded-full" : "rounded-[3px]"}`}
                            style={{ width: p.size, height: p.size, background: p.color }}
                        />
                    ))}
                </div>
            ))}
        </div>
    );
}

========================================================================
FILEPATH: ./src/components/ui/FlapText.tsx
========================================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const GLYPHS = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789";

/**
 * Scrambles through random glyphs and settles left to right, like a
 * split-flap board. `trigger` of 0 means idle; every change above 0 replays.
 */
export function useFlap(text: string, trigger: number, reduce: boolean) {
    const [out, setOut] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (trigger <= 0 || reduce) return;
        let frame = 0;
        const settleAt = (i: number) => 6 + i * 1.6;
        const last = settleAt(text.length) + 2;
        const id = window.setInterval(() => {
            frame += 1;
            setOut(
                text
                    .split("")
                    .map((c, i) =>
                        c === " " || frame >= settleAt(i)
                            ? c
                            : GLYPHS[Math.floor(Math.random() * GLYPHS.length)],
                    )
                    .join(""),
            );
            if (frame >= last) {
                window.clearInterval(id);
                setOut(null);
            }
        }, 42);
        return () => {
            window.clearInterval(id);
            setOut(null);
        };
    }, [text, trigger, reduce]);

    return out ?? text;
}

export default function FlapText({
    text,
    trigger,
    reduce,
    className = "",
}: {
    text: string;
    trigger: number;
    reduce: boolean;
    className?: string;
}) {
    const shown = useFlap(text, trigger, reduce);

    return (
        <span
            role="img"
            aria-label={text}
            className={`inline-flex flex-wrap gap-[0.12em] font-mono ${className}`}
        >
            {shown.split("").map((ch, i) =>
                ch === " " ? (
                    <span key={i} aria-hidden className="w-[0.5em]" />
                ) : (
                    <span
                        key={`${i}-${ch}`}
                        aria-hidden
                        className="rd-flap relative inline-flex h-[1.35em] w-[0.82em] items-center justify-center rounded-[0.18em] bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--background)_14%,transparent)]"
                    >
                        {ch}
                        <span className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 top-1/2 h-px bg-foreground/70" />
                    </span>
                ),
            )}
        </span>
    );
}

"use client";

import { motion, type Variants } from "framer-motion";

const wordStack: Variants = {
    hidden: {},
    show: { transition: { staggerChildren: 0.06, delayChildren: 0.1 } },
};

const wordIn: Variants = {
    hidden: { opacity: 0, y: "0.6em", filter: "blur(8px)" },
    show: {
        opacity: 1,
        y: 0,
        filter: "blur(0px)",
        transition: { type: "spring", stiffness: 200, damping: 20 },
    },
};

const SIZES = {
    md: "text-[clamp(2rem,min(8.5vw,6.2vh),3.5rem)] leading-[1.12] lg:text-[clamp(2.25rem,min(4.2vw,8vh),3.75rem)]",
    xl: "text-[clamp(2.5rem,min(7vw,11vh),5.5rem)] leading-[1.02]",
} as const;

function Word({
    children,
    tilt,
    reduce,
    className = "",
}: {
    children: string;
    tilt: number;
    reduce: boolean;
    className?: string;
}) {
    return (
        <motion.span
            variants={wordIn}
            whileHover={reduce ? undefined : { y: -6, rotate: tilt }}
            whileTap={reduce ? undefined : { scale: 0.92, rotate: -tilt }}
            className={`inline-block cursor-default ${className}`}
        >
            {children}
        </motion.span>
    );
}

function Squiggle({ reduce }: { reduce: boolean }) {
    return (
        <svg
            aria-hidden
            viewBox="0 0 300 14"
            preserveAspectRatio="none"
            className="pointer-events-none absolute -bottom-2 left-0 h-3 w-full text-primary"
        >
            <motion.path
                d="M2 9 C 30 1, 55 1, 80 8 S 130 15, 155 8 S 205 1, 230 8 S 275 14, 298 5"
                fill="none"
                stroke="currentColor"
                strokeWidth="3"
                strokeLinecap="round"
                variants={{
                    hidden: { pathLength: reduce ? 1 : 0 },
                    show: {
                        pathLength: 1,
                        transition: { duration: 0.9, delay: 0.6, ease: [0.65, 0, 0.35, 1] },
                    },
                }}
            />
        </svg>
    );
}

/**
 * Two-line headline. The top line is sans. The bottom line is serif italic in
 * the accent colour, underlined by a hand-drawn squiggle.
 * Needs a parent motion element with "hidden" / "show" variants.
 */
export default function Headline({
    reduce,
    toptext,
    bottomtext,
    size = "md",
}: {
    reduce: boolean;
    toptext: string;
    bottomtext: string;
    size?: keyof typeof SIZES;
}) {
    const top = toptext.split(" ");
    const bottom = bottomtext.split(" ");

    return (
        <motion.h2
            variants={wordStack}
            className={`${SIZES[size]} font-semibold tracking-tight`}
        >
            {top.map((w, i) => (
                <span key={`${w}-${i}`}>
                    <Word tilt={i % 2 ? 3 : -3} reduce={reduce}>
                        {w}
                    </Word>{" "}
                </span>
            ))}
            <span className="relative inline-block">
                {bottom.map((w, i) => (
                    <span key={`${w}-${i}`}>
                        <Word
                            tilt={i % 2 ? -3 : 3}
                            reduce={reduce}
                            className="ds-serif text-[1.08em] text-primary"
                        >
                            {w}
                        </Word>
                        {i < bottom.length - 1 ? " " : ""}
                    </span>
                ))}
                <Squiggle reduce={reduce} />
            </span>
        </motion.h2>
    );
}


========================================================================
FILEPATH: ./src/components/ui/Herotray.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
    AnimatePresence,
    LayoutGroup,
    MotionConfig,
    motion,
    useMotionTemplate,
    useMotionValue,
    useReducedMotion,
    useSpring,
    type Transition,
    type Variants,
} from "framer-motion";
import { HugeiconsIcon } from "@hugeicons/react";
import {
    Download04Icon,
    GithubIcon,
    InstagramIcon,
    Linkedin01Icon,
    Mail01Icon,
    Message01Icon,
    Tick02Icon,
} from "@hugeicons/core-free-icons";
import data from "@/Data/Data.json";

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Config
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const RESUME_URL = "/resume.pdf"; // put your file in /public/resume.pdf
const RESUME_FILENAME = "Raunak-Yadav-Resume.pdf";

const links = data.heroSection.socialLinks;
const isExternal = (href: string) => href.startsWith("http");

// Cell is 44px → 22px = fully round side. Order: TL TR BR BL
const R_FIRST = "22px 12px 12px 22px"; // rounded-l-full, rounded-r-xl
const R_MID = "12px 12px 12px 12px"; //  rounded-xl
const R_LAST = "12px 22px 22px 12px"; //  rounded-r-full, rounded-l-xl

const UI: Transition = { type: "spring", stiffness: 420, damping: 32, mass: 0.7 };
const SOFT: Transition = { type: "spring", stiffness: 260, damping: 26, mass: 0.9 };
const GLIDE: Transition = { type: "spring", stiffness: 520, damping: 38, mass: 0.6 };
const POP: Transition = { type: "spring", stiffness: 600, damping: 18, mass: 0.6 };
const MAGNET = { stiffness: 300, damping: 20, mass: 0.5 };

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Entrance — segments rise, cells pop with a twist, one shimmer sweeps across
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const trayV: Variants = {
    hidden: {},
    show: { transition: { staggerChildren: 0.11, delayChildren: 0.05 } },
};

const segmentV: Variants = {
    hidden: { opacity: 0, y: 22, scale: 0.9, filter: "blur(6px)" },
    show: {
        opacity: 1,
        y: 0,
        scale: 1,
        filter: "blur(0px)",
        transition: { ...SOFT, staggerChildren: 0.06, delayChildren: 0.1 },
    },
};

const cellV: Variants = {
    hidden: { opacity: 0, scale: 0.3, rotate: -40, y: 10 },
    show: { opacity: 1, scale: 1, rotate: 0, y: 0, transition: POP },
};

const sweepV: Variants = {
    hidden: { x: "-120%", opacity: 0 },
    show: {
        x: "260%",
        opacity: [0, 1, 0],
        transition: { duration: 1.1, delay: 0.5, ease: "easeInOut" },
    },
};

// Per-icon personality (rest → hover). Plays while the cell is active.
const ICON_FX: Record<string, Variants> = {
    github: {
        rest: { rotate: 0, scale: 1, y: 0 },
        hover: { rotate: [0, -14, 12, -6, 0], scale: 1.18, y: -1.5, transition: { duration: 0.6 } },
    },
    linkedin: {
        rest: { scale: 1, y: 0 },
        hover: { scale: [1, 1.3, 1.15], y: -2, transition: { duration: 0.45, ease: "easeOut" } },
    },
    instagram: {
        rest: { rotate: 0, scale: 1 },
        hover: { rotate: [0, -12, 90, 0], scale: [1, 1.2, 1.15], transition: { duration: 0.7 } },
    },
    email: {
        rest: { y: 0, scale: 1 },
        hover: { y: [0, -5, 0, -2.5, -1.5], scale: 1.15, transition: { duration: 0.6 } },
    },
    resume: {
        rest: { y: 0 },
        hover: { y: [0, 2.5, 0], transition: { duration: 0.8, repeat: Infinity, ease: "easeInOut" } },
    },
    contact: {
        rest: { rotate: 0, scale: 1, y: 0 },
        hover: { rotate: [0, -14, 10, -5, 0], scale: 1.18, y: -2, transition: { duration: 0.6 } },
    },
};

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Segment — dark pill: cursor spotlight, orbiting border light, entrance sweep
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function Segment({
    className = "",
    children,
    label,
    reduce,
}: {
    className?: string;
    children: React.ReactNode;
    label?: string;
    reduce: boolean;
}) {
    const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const x = useMotionValue(-200);
    const y = useMotionValue(-200);
    const glow = useSpring(0, { stiffness: 200, damping: 26 });
    const bg = useMotionTemplate`radial-gradient(120px circle at ${x}px ${y}px, color-mix(in oklab, var(--primary) 22%, transparent), transparent 70%)`;

    return (
        <motion.div
            ref={ref}
            role={label ? "group" : undefined}
            aria-label={label}
            variants={segmentV}
            onPointerMove={(e) => {
                if (e.pointerType !== "mouse" || !ref.current) return;
                const r = ref.current.getBoundingClientRect();
                x.set(e.clientX - r.left);
                y.set(e.clientY - r.top);
                glow.set(1);
            }}
            onPointerLeave={() => glow.set(0)}
            className={`relative flex items-center gap-1 border border-border/70 bg-muted/40 p-1 backdrop-blur-md ${className}`}
        >
            {/* effects layer — clipped to the pill so the tooltip above isn't */}
            <span
                aria-hidden
                className="pointer-events-none absolute -inset-px overflow-hidden rounded-[inherit]"
            >
                <motion.span
                    style={{ background: bg, opacity: glow }}
                    className="absolute inset-0"
                />

                {/* orbiting light on the border, only while hovered */}
                <motion.span
                    style={{
                        opacity: glow,
                        padding: 1,
                        WebkitMask: "linear-gradient(#000 0 0) content-box, linear-gradient(#000 0 0)",
                        WebkitMaskComposite: "xor",
                        maskComposite: "exclude",
                    }}
                    className="absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden rounded-[inherit]"
                >
                    <motion.span
                        style={{
                            x: "-50%",
                            y: "-50%",
                            background:
                                "conic-gradient(from 0deg, transparent 0 60%, var(--primary) 88%, transparent 100%)",
                        }}
                        animate={reduce ? undefined : { rotate: 360 }}
                        transition={{ duration: 3.2, ease: "linear", repeat: Infinity }}
                        className="absolute left-1/2 top-1/2 aspect-square w-[260%] will-change-transform"
                    />
                </motion.span>

                {/* one-shot entrance shimmer */}
                <motion.span
                    variants={sweepV}
                    className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 w-1/3 -skew-x-12 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-white/25 to-transparent"
                />
            </span>
            {children}
        </motion.div>
    );
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Burst — particles + ring when a download completes
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function Burst() {
    const dots = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => (i / 10) * Math.PI * 2);
    return (
        <span className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center">
            <motion.span
                initial={{ scale: 0.5, opacity: 0.7 }}
                animate={{ scale: 2, opacity: 0 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.7, ease: "easeOut" }}
                className="absolute size-9 rounded-full border border-primary"
            />
            {dots.map((a, i) => (
                <motion.span
                    key={i}
                    initial={{ x: 0, y: 0, scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
                    animate={{
                        x: Math.cos(a) * (i % 2 ? 30 : 22),
                        y: Math.sin(a) * (i % 2 ? 30 : 22),
                        scale: 0,
                        opacity: 0,
                    }}
                    transition={{ duration: 0.65, ease: "easeOut" }}
                    className="absolute size-1.5 rounded-full bg-primary"
                />
            ))}
        </span>
    );
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Cell — magnetic icon button: shared highlight, ripple, tooltip, icon motion
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface CellProps {
    id: string;
    label: string;
    href: string;
    radius: string;
    active: boolean;
    onActive: (id: string | null) => void;
    children: React.ReactNode;
    reduce: boolean;
    tooltip?: string;
    pinTooltip?: boolean;
    overlay?: React.ReactNode;
    anchorProps?: React.ComponentProps<typeof motion.a>;
}

function Cell({
    id,
    label,
    href,
    radius,
    active,
    onActive,
    children,
    reduce,
    tooltip,
    pinTooltip,
    overlay,
    anchorProps,
}: CellProps) {
    const wrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const mx = useSpring(0, MAGNET);
    const my = useSpring(0, MAGNET);
    const [ripples, setRipples] = useState<{ id: number; x: number; y: number }[]>([]);
    const rippleId = useRef(0);
    const showTip = active || !!pinTooltip;

    const onMove = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
        if (reduce || e.pointerType !== "mouse" || !wrapRef.current) return;
        const r = wrapRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
        const dx = e.clientX - (r.left + r.width / 2);
        const dy = e.clientY - (r.top + r.height / 2);
        mx.set(Math.max(-5, Math.min(5, dx * 0.25)));
        my.set(Math.max(-5, Math.min(5, dy * 0.25)));
    };
    const onLeave = () => {
        mx.set(0);
        my.set(0);
    };

    const onDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => {
        navigator.vibrate?.(8);
        if (reduce) return;
        const r = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
        const rid = ++rippleId.current;
        setRipples((p) => [...p, { id: rid, x: e.clientX - r.left, y: e.clientY - r.top }]);
        setTimeout(() => setRipples((p) => p.filter((q) => q.id !== rid)), 650);
    };

    return (
        <motion.div
            ref={wrapRef}
            variants={cellV}
            onPointerMove={onMove}
            onPointerLeave={onLeave}
            className="relative size-11"
        >
            <motion.a
                href={href}
                target={isExternal(href) ? "_blank" : undefined}
                rel={isExternal(href) ? "noopener noreferrer" : undefined}
                aria-label={label}
                data-tray-item
                onPointerEnter={(e) => e.pointerType === "mouse" && onActive(id)}
                onPointerLeave={() => onActive(null)}
                onPointerDown={onDown}
                onFocus={(e) => e.currentTarget.matches(":focus-visible") && onActive(id)}
                onBlur={() => onActive(null)}
                whileTap={{ scale: 0.84 }}
                transition={POP}
                style={{ x: mx, y: my, borderRadius: radius }}
                className={`relative z-10 grid size-full place-items-center overflow-hidden outline-none transition-colors duration-200 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary/70 ${
                    active ? "text-primary" : "text-foreground/70"
                }`}
                {...anchorProps}
            >
                {active && (
                    <motion.span
                        layoutId="tray-highlight"
                        transition={GLIDE}
                        aria-hidden
                        style={{ borderRadius: radius }}
                        className="absolute inset-0 bg-primary/15 shadow-[0_0_18px_-4px_var(--primary)] ring-1 ring-inset ring-primary/30"
                    />
                )}

                {/* click ripples */}
                {ripples.map((r) => (
                    <motion.span
                        key={r.id}
                        aria-hidden
                        initial={{ scale: 0, opacity: 0.4 }}
                        animate={{ scale: 4, opacity: 0 }}
                        transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: "easeOut" }}
                        style={{ left: r.x - 10, top: r.y - 10 }}
                        className="pointer-events-none absolute size-5 rounded-full bg-primary"
                    />
                ))}

                <motion.span
                    variants={ICON_FX[id] ?? ICON_FX.github}
                    initial={false}
                    animate={active ? "hover" : "rest"}
                    className="relative grid place-items-center"
                >
                    {children}
                </motion.span>
            </motion.a>

            {overlay}

            {/* tooltip */}
            <AnimatePresence>
                {showTip && (
                    <motion.span
                        aria-hidden
                        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8, scale: 0.8, rotate: -4 }}
                        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1, rotate: 0 }}
                        exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 4, scale: 0.92, transition: { duration: 0.1 } }}
                        transition={POP}
                        className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-full left-1/2 z-30 mb-3 -translate-x-1/2"
                    >
                        <span className="relative block whitespace-nowrap rounded-full bg-foreground px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-background shadow-lg">
                            <AnimatePresence mode="popLayout" initial={false}>
                                <motion.span
                                    key={tooltip ?? label}
                                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 7, filter: "blur(3px)" }}
                                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, filter: "blur(0px)" }}
                                    exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -7, filter: "blur(3px)" }}
                                    transition={{ duration: 0.16, ease: "easeOut" }}
                                    className="block"
                                >
                                    {tooltip ?? label}
                                </motion.span>
                            </AnimatePresence>
                            <span className="absolute left-1/2 top-full -mt-1 size-2 -translate-x-1/2 rotate-45 rounded-[2px] bg-foreground" />
                        </span>
                    </motion.span>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>
        </motion.div>
    );
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Resume icon — download → progress ring → tick
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

type Status = "idle" | "loading" | "done";

function ResumeIcon({ status }: { status: Status }) {
    return (
        <span className="relative grid size-8 place-items-center">
            <svg className="absolute inset-0 -rotate-90" viewBox="0 0 32 32" aria-hidden>
                <motion.rect
                    x="1"
                    y="1"
                    width="30"
                    height="30"
                    rx="10"
                    fill="none"
                    stroke="var(--primary)"
                    strokeWidth="1.6"
                    strokeLinecap="round"
                    initial={false}
                    animate={{
                        pathLength: status === "idle" ? 0 : status === "loading" ? 0.85 : 1,
                        opacity: status === "idle" ? 0 : 1,
                    }}
                    transition={{ duration: status === "loading" ? 1 : 0.3, ease: "easeOut" }}
                />
            </svg>
            <AnimatePresence mode="wait" initial={false}>
                <motion.span
                    key={status === "done" ? "done" : "dl"}
                    initial={{ scale: 0.3, opacity: 0, rotate: -60 }}
                    animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1, rotate: 0 }}
                    exit={{ scale: 0.3, opacity: 0, rotate: 60 }}
                    transition={POP}
                    className="grid place-items-center"
                >
                    <motion.span
                        animate={status === "loading" ? { y: [0, 3, 0] } : { y: 0 }}
                        transition={
                            status === "loading"
                                ? { duration: 0.6, repeat: Infinity, ease: "easeInOut" }
                                : { duration: 0.2 }
                        }
                        className="grid place-items-center"
                    >
                        <HugeiconsIcon
                            icon={status === "done" ? Tick02Icon : Download04Icon}
                            size={20}
                            strokeWidth={1.8}
                        />
                    </motion.span>
                </motion.span>
            </AnimatePresence>
        </span>
    );
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Tray — [ GitHub · LinkedIn · Instagram · Email ] [ Resume ] [ Contact ]
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const SOCIALS = [
    { id: "github", label: "GitHub", href: links.githubUrl, icon: GithubIcon },
    { id: "linkedin", label: "LinkedIn", href: links.linkedInUrl, icon: Linkedin01Icon },
    { id: "instagram", label: "Instagram", href: links.instagramUrl, icon: InstagramIcon },
    { id: "email", label: "Email", href: links.gmailUrl, icon: Mail01Icon },
];

export default function HeroTray({ delay = 1.2 }: { delay?: number }) {
    const reduce = !!useReducedMotion();
    const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
    const [active, setActive] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const trayRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    const timers = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>[]>([]);
    const [status, setStatus] = useState<Status>("idle");

    useEffect(() => {
        if (reduce) return;
        const t = setTimeout(() => setReady(true), delay * 1000);
        return () => clearTimeout(t);
    }, [delay, reduce]);

    useEffect(() => () => timers.current.forEach(clearTimeout), []);

    const onResumeClick = () => {
        if (status !== "idle") return;
        setStatus("loading");
        timers.current.push(
            setTimeout(() => setStatus("done"), 1100),
            setTimeout(() => setStatus("idle"), 3000),
        );
    };

    // ← / → move between every cell in the tray (Home / End jump to the ends)
    const onKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
        const keys = ["ArrowRight", "ArrowLeft", "Home", "End"];
        if (!keys.includes(e.key) || !trayRef.current) return;
        const items = Array.from(trayRef.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLAnchorElement>("a[data-tray-item]"));
        const i = items.indexOf(document.activeElement as HTMLAnchorElement);
        if (i === -1) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        const next =
            e.key === "Home"
                ? 0
                : e.key === "End"
                  ? items.length - 1
                  : (i + (e.key === "ArrowRight" ? 1 : -1) + items.length) % items.length;
        items[next].focus();
    };

    const resumeTip =
        status === "loading" ? "Downloading…" : status === "done" ? "Downloaded" : "Resume";

    return (
        <MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">
            <LayoutGroup id="hero-tray">
                <motion.div
                    ref={trayRef}
                    onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
                    variants={trayV}
                    initial={reduce ? false : "hidden"}
                    animate={reduce || ready ? "show" : "hidden"}
                    className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-1.5 sm:flex-nowrap"
                >
                    {/* 1 · socials — rounded left */}
                    <Segment
                        label="Social links"
                        reduce={reduce}
                        className="rounded-l-full rounded-r-2xl"
                    >
                        {SOCIALS.map((s, i) => (
                            <Cell
                                key={s.id}
                                id={s.id}
                                label={s.label}
                                href={s.href}
                                radius={i === 0 ? R_FIRST : R_MID}
                                active={active === s.id}
                                onActive={setActive}
                                reduce={reduce}
                            >
                                <HugeiconsIcon icon={s.icon} size={20} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                            </Cell>
                        ))}
                    </Segment>

                    {/* 2 · resume */}
                    <Segment reduce={reduce} className="">
                        <Cell
                            id="resume"
                            label="Download resume"
                            tooltip={resumeTip}
                            pinTooltip={status !== "idle"}
                            href={RESUME_URL}
                            radius={R_MID}
                            active={active === "resume"}
                            onActive={setActive}
                            reduce={reduce}
                            overlay={status === "done" ? <Burst key="burst" /> : null}
                            anchorProps={{ download: RESUME_FILENAME, onClick: onResumeClick }}
                        >
                            <ResumeIcon status={status} />
                        </Cell>
                        <span className="sr-only" aria-live="polite">
                            {status === "loading"
                                ? "Downloading resume"
                                : status === "done"
                                  ? "Resume downloaded"
                                  : ""}
                        </span>
                    </Segment>

                    {/* 3 · contact — rounded right */}
                    <Segment reduce={reduce} className="rounded-l-2xl rounded-r-full">
                        <Cell
                            id="contact"
                            label="Contact"
                            href={links.gmailUrl}
                            radius={R_LAST}
                            active={active === "contact"}
                            onActive={setActive}
                            reduce={reduce}
                        >
                            <HugeiconsIcon icon={Message01Icon} size={20} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                        </Cell>
                    </Segment>
                </motion.div>
            </LayoutGroup>
        </MotionConfig>
    );
}

import type { CSSProperties } from "react";

/** Endless tech ticker. Pauses on hover. Static and wrapped with reduced motion. */
export default function Marquee({
    items,
    duration = 48,
}: {
    items: string[];
    duration?: number;
}) {
    const group = (dup: boolean) => (
        <ul
            className="flex shrink-0 items-center"
            aria-hidden={dup || undefined}
            data-dup={dup || undefined}
        >
            {items.map((item, i) => (
                <li key={`${item}-${i}`} className="flex items-center">
                    <span className="ds-eyebrow px-6 text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground">
                        {item}
                    </span>
                    <span aria-hidden className="text-primary">✦</span>
                </li>
            ))}
        </ul>
    );

    return (
        <div
            role="group"
            aria-label="Technologies I work with"
            className="ds-marquee relative overflow-hidden border-y border-border bg-background py-4 [mask-image:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,black_8%,black_92%,transparent)]"
        >
            <div
                className="ds-marquee-track"
                style={{ "--ds-marquee-duration": `${duration}s` } as CSSProperties}
            >
                {group(false)}
                {group(true)}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

FILEPATH: ./src/components/ui/PlayButton.tsx

// redesign.sh:v1
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";

type PlayProps = {
    href: string;
    children: ReactNode;
    /** "solid" takes the surrounding --tint (or the accent). "outline" is the hairline pill. */
    variant?: "outline" | "solid";
    external?: boolean;
    round?: boolean;
    ariaLabel?: string;
};

/**
 * Compatibility adapter. The neo-brutalist offset-shadow button is gone; every
 * existing <PlayButton> now renders the site's custom Button (label + arrow chip).
 */
export default function PlayButton({ href, children, variant = "outline", external = false, round = false, ariaLabel }: PlayProps) {
    return (
        <Button
            href={href}
            variant={variant}
            external={external ? true : undefined}
            iconOnly={round}
            ariaLabel={ariaLabel}
        >
            {children}
        </Button>
    );
}

import type { ReactNode } from "react";

/**
 * Full-height section. It is at least one viewport tall and grows with its

 */
export default function Screen({
    id,
    as: Tag = "section",
    children,
    className = "",
}: {
    id?: string;
    as?: "section" | "footer";
    children: ReactNode;
    className?: string;
}) {
    return (
        <Tag
            id={id}
            className={`relative box-border flex min-h-svh w-full max-w-[100vw] flex-col overflow-x-clip border-t border-border bg-background ${className}`}
        >
            {children}
        </Tag>
    );
}

"use client";

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import Eyebrow from "@/components/ui/Eyebrow";
import Headline from "@/components/ui/Headline";
import { fade, stackLoose, viewportOnce } from "@/lib/motion";

/** Shared header: numbered eyebrow, headline, optional control on the right. */
export default function SectionHeader({
    index,
    label,
    top,
    bottom,
    reduce,
    aside,
    className = "",
}: {
    index: string;
    label: string;
    top: string;
    bottom: string;
    reduce: boolean;
    aside?: ReactNode;
    className?: string;
}) {
    return (
        <motion.header
            initial={reduce ? "show" : "hidden"}
            whileInView="show"
            viewport={viewportOnce}
            variants={stackLoose}
            className={`flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-end lg:justify-between ${className}`}
        >
            <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3">
                <motion.div variants={fade}>
                    <Eyebrow index={index}>{label}</Eyebrow>
                </motion.div>
                <Headline reduce={reduce} toptext={top} bottomtext={bottom} />
            </div>

            {aside && (
                <motion.div variants={fade} className="min-w-0 max-w-full lg:pb-1.5">
                    {aside}
                </motion.div>
            )}
        </motion.header>
    );
}

FILEPATH: ./src/components/ui/Shapes.tsx
